# I06b review log

## Plan review dispositions (Codex Sol 6.1 xhigh, 2026-11-02)

Prompt: `plan-review-prompt.md` (also /tmp/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02-plan-review.md). Raw final message: `plan-review-raw.txt` (full transcript /tmp/niche-fe-plan-review-raw.txt). Verdict: revise before implementation. All 14 findings accepted; PLAN.md is now v2.

| # | Sev | Finding | Disposition |
|---|-----|---------|-------------|
| 1 | P1 | far-field-energy cannot make the gate `supported` (ablation only converts P to C, so D_poly = 0) | Accepted. Fixture now tests the inoculum contrast alone (`D_inoc` fooled, `D_inoc2` not); full gate rejected via D_poly = 0 and recorded; full-gate trap deferred (PLAN 2.1, 4.5, 11). |
| 2 | P1 | credit direction reversed; zero residual is an identity | Accepted. Credit dropped; signed `constructionGap = total(b) - total(f)` with stated sign meaning; all comparators enter the max (gap < 0 is the conservative side); physical conservation is each arm's own ledger (PLAN 4.1). |
| 3 | P1 | unchanged transfer audit cannot recognize a credit | Accepted by removal: no credit in the audit; per-arm own baseline, optional `baseline` param if needed, serialization-unchanged test (PLAN 3, 7). |
| 4 | P1 | G7 contradicts source-manifest checks | Accepted. Manifest-only re-pin of rung-1 `manifest.json` allowed (I08 precedent); other rung-1 files byte-identical; gate changed to `git diff --name-only` must list only manifest.json (PLAN 3, 6, 8). |
| 5 | P1 | positive control uses allowP placement real assay forbids | Accepted. Qualifying control must use ordinary placement; allowP walls diagnostic only; if no legal geometry, control unavailable and rung ends unsupported-measurement (PLAN 4.3). |
| 6 | P2 | fB loses free E through the d1B debit | Accepted. fB omits the lattice debit; free E equals b cell for cell before and after placement; extra bound energy shows as gap < 0 (PLAN 4.1, 7). |
| 7 | P2 | replicate seeds outside reservation | Accepted. Reserve contiguous 8,601,001-8,608,999 (R up to 8), collision scan over the enumerated list, protocol hash binds it (PLAN 5.1, 7). |
| 8 | P2 | free-gap-replay reversed direction | Accepted. Direction corrected to d0 MORE free E (22,169 vs 9,917) (PLAN 4.1, 4.5). |
| 9 | P2 | stopped outcome cannot produce promised artifacts | Accepted. `--write` always seals a payload (stopped if nothing qualifies); only fresh simulation is gated; artifact tests run after generation (PLAN 7, 8). |
| 10 | P2 | G3 / G-NOISE not one reproducible rule | Accepted. Per candidate, limiting gating contrast in summed-R units, wall pass rule 7 of 8 and median >= delta_c, ladder adds 60, doses strictly <= candidate ceiling (PLAN 4.2, 4.3). |
| 11 | P2 | noise gate does not establish power | Accepted. Power statement added (k >= 13; 40.5% at 0.75, 79% at 0.85, 93% at 0.90, 99% at 0.95); a negative is bounded to "no benefit >= delta in about 90% of blocks" (PLAN 4.2, 5.3). |
| 12 | P2 | freeze not enforced by existing lock | Accepted. Lock also binds niche source hashes; `--write` and `--replay-check` verify lock, clean tree and pushed identical lock via `git ls-tree origin/...` before any fresh seed; guard test (PLAN 9, 7). |
| 13 | P2 | survival rule cannot support survival claim | Accepted. Survival recast as field-arm viability (14 of 16); paired survival contrast attribution only; claim limited to biomass benefit (PLAN 1, 4.2, 5.3). |
| 14 | P2 | budget omits dominant work | Accepted. Full workload enumerated (wall ladder worst case about 184k steps), calibration reused at write, builder dose-response dropped, cut order stated (PLAN 8 G6, 11). |

Reviewer confirmations kept: shared Stage-1 histories are valid paired blocks (sum over R does not inflate n); absolute-T1 alignment supported by the code; founder scaling needs no WorldConfig key (knob dropped anyway). Seed ranges in the original plan had no collisions with the current registry.

## Freeze review dispositions (Codex Sol 6.1 xhigh, 2026-11-02, before the freeze commit)

Prompt: `freeze-review-prompt.md`. Raw output (full transcript incl. tool reads): `freeze-review-raw.txt`. Verdict: the stopped outcome is justified; freeze enforcement needed fixes. All 8 findings accepted and addressed before the freeze commit; no fresh seed had been read. A second review pass of the fixes was not run; the guard fixes are covered by stubbed-git tests instead.

| # | Sev | Finding | Disposition |
|---|-----|---------|-------------|
| 1 | P1 | guard read a local `origin/...` tracking ref, not the remote | Fixed. `freezeGuard` runs `git ls-remote origin refs/heads/<branch>`, takes the remote commit, requires it in the local object store, and compares that commit's blobs with `git hash-object`; stubbed tests for no remote branch, missing local commit, dirty file, missing or differing blob. |
| 2 | P1 | exported `generateFe()` bypassed the guard | Fixed. `generateFe(git)` calls `guarded()` first; `--write` and `--replay-check` reach simulation only through it; test: it rejects (stubbed git) before any simulation. |
| 3 | P2 | PLAN.md and Amendment 3 outside the lock | Fixed. `planSha256` in the lock, PLAN.md in `frozenPaths` (so also in the pushed-identical set), `verifyLock(planPath)` test that a document-only change fails. PLAN.md is therefore not edited after the freeze commit. |
| 4 | P2 | power wording overclaimed an exclusion bound; Holm threshold depends on candidate count | Fixed in `PROTOCOL_FE.powerStatement`, `LIMITS_FE`, the page disclosure and PLAN 4.2: a negative is a failure to detect under these rules; 13 of 16 with three candidates, 12 with one. |
| 5 | P2 | ladder stop rule ignored G-NOISE | Fixed. `settled()` (qualified, or passing dose known and all within-ceiling doses run); `runLadder(deltas, ceilings, ...)`; unit test with the reviewer's counterexample. Calibration re-run: byte-identical to the first run apart from the new field below. |
| 6 | P3 | fB `gap < 0` claim wrong (gap to b stayed positive in dev) | Fixed in code comment, PLAN 4.1 and the test (asserts `gap(fS) - gap(fB) = (eB - eA) * dB`). |
| 7 | P3 | `inoc2 <= inoc` by construction, so "did not rescue" was predetermined | Fixed. Added reported-only `inocMatched = Y(b) - max(fU, fS, fB)`; PLAN section 10 now says `inoc2` equalled `inoc` in 24 of 24 dev blocks and reports `inocMatched` (zero or negative in 21, positive in 3). The registered gate is unchanged. |
| 8 | P3 | wrong numeric summaries in Amendment 3 | Fixed: floor reductions 15.5%, 11.4%, 27.7%; best weakest-contrast median 1.5 (space contrast best 29.5); all recomputed from `calibration.json`. |

Reviewer confirmations kept: the lock matched all 38 sources, calibration, reservation digest and 176 enumerated seeds; saved sums, contrasts, deltas, ceilings and wall summaries agree; W2 reproduces zero placeable cells; the stopped report gives three `unsupported-measurement` labels and an empty negative list; free-E matching, signed-gap arithmetic, shared placement and audited conservation correct; physics packages, golden pins, `RULE_VERSION` and `defaultConfig` untouched; rung-1 `--check` passes.

     1	// Pure helpers for the second-rung section of the niche page (free-energy-matched comparators, noise vs effect, dose ladder, edge per
     2	// candidate, far-field-energy panel). Reads the sealed second-rung evidence only and runs no simulation.
     3	import { edgeOf, esc, fmt, half, ratText, type Rational } from './render.ts';
     4	
     5	interface FreeRowView { id: string; freeE: string; total: string; constructionGap: string; flag: string }
     6	interface BlockFeView { seed: number; valid?: boolean; freeBefore?: FreeRowView[]; freeAfter?: FreeRowView[]; invalidReason?: string | null }
     7	interface DoseStat { k: number; p: Rational; medianTimes2: number }
     8	interface DoseRowView { totalP: number; seeds: readonly number[]; stats: Record<string, DoseStat>; limiting: string; limitingMedianTimes2: number }
     9	interface QualView { cluster: number; delta: number; ceiling: number; minDetectableDose: number | null; qualifyingDose: number | null; qualified: boolean; reason: string }
    10	interface ResultFeView {
    11	  id: string; label: string; simulated: boolean; mechanism: string; reasons: string[]; pCand: Rational; holmThreshold: Rational | null;
    12	  gating: Record<string, { k: number; n: number; p: Rational; medianTimes2: number; passes: boolean }>;
    13	  reported: { inoc: { k: number; n: number; medianTimes2: number }; naive: { k: number; n: number; medianTimes2: number } };
    14	}
    15	interface FixtureFeView { id: string; seeds: readonly number[]; naiveK: number; naiveWouldSay: string; inocFooledOld: boolean; inocCaughtNew: boolean; kOld: number; kNew: number; gatingK: Record<string, number>; caughtBy: string[]; fullGateLabel: string; freeBefore: FreeRowView[] }
    16	export interface EvidenceFeView {
    17	  protocolHash: string; integritySha256: string; amendments?: string[]; powerStatement: string; limits: string[];
    18	  protocol: { R: number; freshSeeds: readonly number[]; wallSeeds: readonly number[] };
    19	  dev: { R: number; candidates: { cluster: number; blocks: BlockFeView[]; deltaProposed: number; builderPMedianTimes2: number }[]; ladder: { rows: DoseRowView[]; w2: { status: string; reason: string } }; qualification: QualView[] };
    20	  fresh: { ran: boolean; seeds: readonly number[]; candidates: { cluster: number; blocks: BlockFeView[]; medianYTimes2: Record<string, number | null>; survivalPositiveBlocks: number; input: { viableBlocks: number; expectedBlocks: number } }[]; results: ResultFeView[]; panel: { verdict: string; text: string }; caveats: string[] };
    21	  fixtures: { reports: FixtureFeView[]; freeGap: { seed: number; d0FreeE: string; bFreeE: string; d0Total: string; bTotal: string }[]; guards: { id: string; ok: boolean }[] };
    22	}
    23	
    24	export const COMPARATOR_TEXT: Record<string, string> = {
    25	  b: 'Builder removed (the field arm)',
    26	  d0: 'Receiver alone, total energy matched',
    27	  d1E: 'Receiver alone + field energy near the inoculum',
    28	  d1B: 'Receiver alone + field matter near the inoculum',
    29	  fU: 'Free-energy matched, spread uniformly',
    30	  fS: 'Free-energy matched, same pattern cell for cell',
    31	  fB: 'Free-energy matched + the field\'s biomass',
    32	};
    33	const FREE_ORDER = ['b', 'd0', 'd1E', 'd1B', 'fU', 'fS', 'fB'];
    34	const GAP_WORD: Record<string, string> = { 'comparator-deficit': 'holds less total energy than the field', 'comparator-excess': 'holds more total energy than the field', 'total-matched': 'total energy equal', reference: 'reference' };
    35	
    36	/** Free E and total energy per comparator for one block: the d0-versus-b gap (the 9,917 vs 22,169 style numbers) and what matching does to it. */
    37	export function freeEnergyHtml(rows: FreeRowView[] | undefined, seed: number | undefined): string {
    38	  if (!rows?.length) return '<tr><td colspan="4">No block carries a free-energy table.</td></tr>';
    39	  const by = new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.id, r]));
    40	  return FREE_ORDER.filter((id) => by.has(id)).map((id) => {
    41	    const r = by.get(id)!;
    42	    return `<tr><th scope="row">${esc(COMPARATOR_TEXT[id] ?? id)}</th><td>${fmt(Number(r.freeE))}</td><td>${fmt(Number(r.total))}</td><td>${r.id === 'b' ? 'reference' : `${esc(fmt(Number(r.constructionGap)))}: ${esc(GAP_WORD[r.flag] ?? r.flag)}`}</td></tr>`;
    43	  }).join('') + (seed === undefined ? '' : `<tr><td colspan="4" class="muted">Block seed ${seed}, before the inoculum is placed. Gap = total(field arm) minus total(comparator).</td></tr>`);
    44	}
    45	
    46	export function noiseEffectHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    47	  const rows = ev.dev.qualification.map((q) => {
    48	    const row = ev.dev.ladder.rows.find((r) => r.totalP === q.qualifyingDose) ?? ev.dev.ladder.rows.find((r) => r.totalP === q.minDetectableDose) ?? ev.dev.ladder.rows.reduce<DoseRowView | undefined>((m, r) => (!m || r.limitingMedianTimes2 > m.limitingMedianTimes2 ? r : m), undefined);
    49	    const effect = row ? row.limitingMedianTimes2 / 2 : null;
    50	    const max = Math.max(1, q.delta, effect ?? 0);
    51	    const bar = (v: number | null, cls: string) => `<span class="${cls}" style="width:${Math.round((100 * Math.max(0, v ?? 0)) / max)}%"></span>`;
    52	    return `<tr><th scope="row">cluster ${q.cluster}</th><td>${fmt(q.delta)}</td><td>${effect === null ? 'n/a' : half(row!.limitingMedianTimes2)}${row ? ` at dose ${fmt(row.totalP)} (${esc(row.limiting)}); ${q.qualified || q.minDetectableDose !== null ? 'wall detected' : 'wall not detected at any dose, best dose shown'}` : ''}</td>
    53	      <td><div class="bar" role="img" aria-label="noise floor delta ${fmt(q.delta)} against the wall effect ${effect === null ? 'not detected' : fmt(effect)}">${bar(q.delta, 'bar-noise')}</div><div class="bar">${bar(effect, 'bar-effect')}</div></td></tr>`;
    54	  });
    55	  return rows.join('');
    56	}
    57	
    58	export function doseLadderHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    59	  const n = ev.protocol.wallSeeds.length;
    60	  const rows = ev.dev.ladder.rows.map((r) => {
    61	    const gk = Object.entries(r.stats).map(([k, s]) => `${esc(k)} ${s.k}/${n}`).join(', ');
    62	    const passes = ev.dev.qualification.map((q) => `c${q.cluster}: ${r.totalP <= q.ceiling ? 'within ceiling' : 'above ceiling'}`).join('; ');
    63	    return `<tr><th scope="row">W1 ring, polymer ${fmt(r.totalP)}</th><td>${gk}</td><td>${esc(r.limiting)} ${half(r.limitingMedianTimes2)}</td><td>${esc(passes)}</td></tr>`;
    64	  });
    65	  rows.push(`<tr><th scope="row">W2 floor under the receiver</th><td colspan="3">${esc(ev.dev.ladder.w2.status)}: ${esc(ev.dev.ladder.w2.reason)}</td></tr>`);
    66	  return rows.join('');
    67	}
    68	
    69	export function qualificationHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    70	  return ev.dev.qualification.map((q) => `<tr><th scope="row">cluster ${q.cluster}</th><td>${fmt(q.ceiling)}</td><td>${q.minDetectableDose === null ? 'none on the ladder' : fmt(q.minDetectableDose)}</td><td>${q.qualified ? `qualified at ${fmt(q.qualifyingDose)}` : 'not qualified'}</td><td>${esc(q.reason)}</td></tr>`).join('');
    71	}
    72	
    73	export function edgeFeHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    74	  return ev.fresh.results.map((r) => {
    75	    const e = edgeOf(r.label, r.mechanism);
    76	    const g = ['builder', 'poly', 'space', 'inoc2'].map((k) => (r.simulated ? `${esc(k)} ${r.gating[k].k}/${r.gating[k].n}${r.gating[k].passes ? '' : ' (no)'}` : `${esc(k)} not run`)).join(', ');
    77	    const word = r.simulated ? e.text : 'Not simulated: the positive control did not qualify this builder, so there is no negative and no positive here.';
    78	    return `<li class="edge-fe edge-${e.state}"><strong>${esc(r.id.replace('cluster-', 'Builder cluster '))}: edge ${esc(e.word)}.</strong> ${esc(word)}<br><span class="muted">Label <code>${esc(r.label)}</code>${r.reasons.length ? ` (${esc(r.reasons.join('; '))})` : ''}. Blocks positive: ${g}.</span></li>`;
    79	  }).join('');
    80	}
    81	
    82	export function farFieldHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    83	  const rows = ev.fixtures.reports.map((f) => `<tr><th scope="row">${esc(f.id)}</th><td>${esc(f.naiveWouldSay)} (${f.naiveK} of ${f.seeds.length})</td><td>${f.kOld} of ${f.seeds.length}: ${f.inocFooledOld ? 'fooled' : 'not fooled'}</td><td>${f.kNew} of ${f.seeds.length}: ${f.inocCaughtNew ? 'caught' : 'not caught'}</td><td>${esc(f.fullGateLabel)}${f.caughtBy.length ? ` (rejected by ${esc(f.caughtBy.join(', '))})` : ''}</td></tr>`);
    84	  return rows.join('');
    85	}
    86	
    87	export function verdictFeHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    88	  const f = ev.fresh;
    89	  const stopped = f.ran ? '' : '<p class="verdict-stop"><strong>Stopped before the fresh-seed run.</strong> No fresh block was simulated, so there are no per-candidate contrasts: this states how sensitive the assay is at realistic polymer doses, not that no niche is built.</p>';
    90	  return `<p class="verdict-text"><strong>${esc(f.panel.verdict.replaceAll('-', ' '))}.</strong> ${esc(f.panel.text)}</p>${stopped}
    91	    <ul>${f.caveats.map((c) => `<li>${esc(c)}</li>`).join('')}</ul>
    92	    <p class="muted">Protocol hash <code>${esc(ev.protocolHash.slice(0, 16))}</code>, seal <code>${esc(ev.integritySha256.slice(0, 16))}</code>. Each arm summed over ${ev.protocol.R} replicate sims; ${f.ran ? `${f.seeds.length} fresh blocks per qualified candidate` : 'no fresh blocks'}; ${ev.dev.candidates[0]?.blocks.length ?? 0} dev blocks per candidate were used for calibration only.</p>`;
    93	}
    94	
    95	export function pendingHtml(): string {
    96	  return '<p class="muted">The second-rung evidence has not been written yet (docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/evidence.json). Run <code>node tools/niche/niche.ts --rung fe --write</code> after the freeze.</p>';
    97	}
    98	
    99	/** First block that carries a free-energy table, from the fresh blocks if any ran, otherwise dev. */
   100	export function firstFreeBlock(ev: EvidenceFeView): { rows: FreeRowView[] | undefined; seed: number | undefined } {
   101	  const b = ev.fresh.candidates[0]?.blocks[0] ?? ev.dev.candidates[0]?.blocks[0];
   102	  return { rows: b?.freeBefore, seed: b?.seed };
   103	}

    39	  const paths = ['a.ts', 'b.ts'];
    40	  const git = (over: Partial<Record<string, string>> = {}): Git => (args) => {
    41	    if (args[0] === 'status') return over.status ?? '';
    42	    if (args[0] === 'ls-remote') { if (over.remote === 'throw') throw new Error('no such ref'); return over.remote ?? `${'a'.repeat(40)}\trefs/heads/${BRANCH}\n`; }
    43	    if (args[0] === 'ls-tree') { if (over.tree === 'throw') throw new Error('bad ref'); expect(args[2]).toBe('a'.repeat(40)); return over.tree ?? '100644 blob aaa\ta.ts\n100644 blob bbb\tb.ts\n'; }
    44	    if (args[0] === 'hash-object') return over.hash ?? 'aaa\nbbb\n';
    45	    throw new Error(args.join(' '));
    46	  };
    47	  it('allows an identical, clean, pushed freeze', () => expect(freezeGuard(paths, git())).toEqual([]));
    48	  it('refuses an uncommitted frozen file', () => expect(freezeGuard(paths, git({ status: ' M a.ts\n' })).join()).toMatch(/not committed clean/));
    49	  it('refuses when the remote has no such branch (the remote is asked, not a local tracking ref)', () => expect(freezeGuard(paths, git({ remote: 'throw' })).join()).toMatch(/push the freeze commit first/));
    50	  it('refuses when the remote commit is not available locally', () => expect(freezeGuard(paths, git({ tree: 'throw' })).join()).toMatch(/git fetch/));
    51	  it('the generator itself refuses an unpublished or unverifiable freeze before simulating anything', async () => {

    54	  });
    55	  it('refuses a file absent from, or different on, the pushed branch', () => {

     1	// I06b evidence assembly: dev (calibration) report, fresh (confirmation) report for qualified candidates only, fixtures, guards, the
     2	// decision and the seal. Pure and deterministic; integers, integer rationals and decimal strings only. `--write` and `--replay-check`
     3	// load the frozen calibration (dev) and regenerate only the fresh blocks and the fixtures.
     4	import { canonicalJSON, sha256HexJS } from "@bl/schema";
     5	import { medianTimes2 } from "./decision.ts";
     6	import { decideCandidatesFe, panelVerdictFe, type CandidateInputFe, type CandidateResultFe, type PanelResultFe } from "./decisionFe.ts";
     7	import { deltaOf } from "./evidence.ts";
     8	import { runLadder, qualify, type LadderReport, type Qualification } from "./dose.ts";
     9	import { fieldBlock, fixtureProtocolFe, decideFixtureFe, freeGapSeed, nullBlockFe, runGuardsFe, takeoverBlockFe, wallGateBlockFe2, type FieldVariant, type FixtureReportFe, type FreeGapSeed, type GuardFe } from "./fixturesFe.ts";
    10	import { GATING_FE, PROTOCOL_FE, enumerateSeedsFe, protocolHashFe, type ProtocolFe } from "./protocol.ts";

    13	export const EVIDENCE_VERSION_FE = "niche-fe-evidence-v1";

    15	export interface DevCandidateFe {
    16	  cluster: number;
    17	  blocks: BlockResultFe[];
    18	  noise: { absBuilder: number[]; absNonbuilder: number[] };
    19	  deltaProposed: number;
    20	  /** Twice the median over dev blocks of the builder history's total polymer at the end of conditioning (also the realistic ceiling 2 x median). */
    21	  builderPMedianTimes2: number;
    22	}
    23	
    24	export interface DevReportFe {
    25	  purpose: "calibration";
    26	  R: number;
    27	  seeds: readonly number[];
    28	  candidates: DevCandidateFe[];
    29	  ladder: LadderReport;
    30	  qualification: Qualification[];
    31	}
    32	
    33	export function devReportFe(p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE, log: (s: string) => void = () => {}): DevReportFe {
    34	  const candidates: DevCandidateFe[] = p.panel.map((cluster) => {
    35	    const blocks = p.devSeeds.map((s) => runBlockFe(cluster, s, p));
    36	    const invalid = blocks.filter((b) => !b.valid);
    37	    if (invalid.length) throw new Error(`cluster ${cluster}: invalid dev block(s) ${invalid.map((b) => `${b.seed} (${b.invalidReason})`).join(", ")}`);
    38	    const noiseBlocks = p.devReplicateSeeds.map((s) => noiseBlock(cluster, s, p));
    39	    const noise = { absBuilder: noiseBlocks.map((n) => n.absBuilder), absNonbuilder: noiseBlocks.map((n) => n.absNonbuilder) };
    40	    const pMed2 = medianTimes2(blocks.map((b) => Number(b.costs.find((c) => c.kind === "builder")?.P ?? 0)));
    41	    const deltaProposed = deltaOf(p.reserve.B * p.R, [...noise.absBuilder, ...noise.absNonbuilder]);
    42	    log(`cluster ${cluster}: delta ${deltaProposed}, builder P median x2 ${pMed2}`);
    43	    return { cluster, blocks, noise, deltaProposed, builderPMedianTimes2: pMed2 };
    44	  });
    45	  const deltas = Object.fromEntries(candidates.map((c) => [String(c.cluster), c.deltaProposed]));
    46	  const ceilings = Object.fromEntries(candidates.map((c) => [String(c.cluster), c.builderPMedianTimes2]));
    47	  const ladder = runLadder(deltas, ceilings, p, (row) => log(`dose ${row.totalP}: ${GATING_FE.map((k) => `${k} ${row.stats[k].k}/${p.wallSeeds.length} med ${row.stats[k].medianTimes2 / 2}`).join(", ")}`));
    48	  const qualification = candidates.map((c) => qualify(c.cluster, c.deltaProposed, c.builderPMedianTimes2, ladder.rows, p));
    49	  return { purpose: "calibration", R: p.R, seeds: p.devSeeds, candidates, ladder, qualification };
    50	}
    51	
    52	export interface FreshCandidateFe {
    53	  cluster: number;
    54	  blocks: BlockResultFe[];
    55	  input: CandidateInputFe;
    56	  /** Per arm: twice the median of Y (sum over replicates) over the fresh blocks. */
    57	  medianYTimes2: Record<string, number | null>;
    58	  survivedByArm: Record<string, { reps: number; of: number }>;
    59	  /** Paired survival contrast: blocks where the field arm had strictly more surviving replicates than the best comparator (attribution only). */
    60	  survivalPositiveBlocks: number;
    61	  fluxAttribution: { available: boolean; reason: string; growthAttributedBlocks: number; coCulture: "unavailable" };
    62	  /** Secondary, never gating: twice the median of the per-arm AUC. */
    63	  aucMedianTimes2: Record<string, number | null>;
    64	}
    65	
    66	export interface FreshReportFe {
    67	  purpose: "confirmation";
    68	  seeds: readonly number[];
    69	  /** false when no candidate qualified: no fresh seed was simulated. */
    70	  ran: boolean;
    71	  candidates: FreshCandidateFe[];
    72	  inputs: CandidateInputFe[];
    73	  results: CandidateResultFe[];
    74	  panel: PanelResultFe;
    75	  caveats: string[];
    76	}
    77	
    78	const med = (xs: readonly number[]): number | null => (xs.length ? medianTimes2(xs) : null);
    79	
    80	export function freshReportFe(dev: DevReportFe, p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE, log: (s: string) => void = () => {}): FreshReportFe {
    81	  const n = p.freshSeeds.length;
    82	  const zeros = Array(n).fill(0);
    83	  const candidates: FreshCandidateFe[] = [];
    84	  const inputs: CandidateInputFe[] = [];
    85	  for (const q of dev.qualification) {
    86	    const delta = p.delta[String(q.cluster)];
    87	    if (!q.qualified) {
    88	      inputs.push({ id: `cluster-${q.cluster}`, status: "not-simulated", invalidReasons: [], contrasts: { builder: zeros, poly: zeros, space: zeros, inoc2: zeros, spaceP: zeros, spaceNP: zeros, inoc: zeros, inocMatched: zeros, naive: zeros }, delta, viableBlocks: 0, blocks: n, expectedBlocks: n, notSimulatedReasons: [q.reason] });
    89	      continue;
    90	    }
    91	    const raw = p.freshSeeds.map((s) => runBlockFe(q.cluster, s, p));
    92	    log(`cluster ${q.cluster}: fresh ${raw.filter((b) => b.valid).length}/${n} valid`);
    93	    const col = (k: keyof ContrastsFe) => raw.map((b) => (b.contrasts ? b.contrasts[k] : 0));
    94	    const valid = raw.filter((b) => b.valid).length;
    95	    const input: CandidateInputFe = {
    96	      id: `cluster-${q.cluster}`,
    97	      status: valid === n ? "ok" : "invalid",
    98	      invalidReasons: raw.filter((b) => !b.valid).map((b) => `seed ${b.seed}: ${b.invalidReason}`),
    99	      contrasts: { builder: col("builder"), poly: col("poly"), space: col("space"), inoc2: col("inoc2"), spaceP: col("spaceP"), spaceNP: col("spaceNP"), inoc: col("inoc"), inocMatched: col("inocMatched"), naive: col("naive") },
   100	      delta,
   101	      viableBlocks: raw.filter((b) => b.viable).length,
   102	      blocks: valid,
   103	      expectedBlocks: n,
   104	      notSimulatedReasons: [],
   105	    };
   106	    inputs.push(input);
   107	    const sat = raw.filter((b) => Object.values(b.outcomes).some((o) => o.saturated)).length;
   108	    candidates.push({
   109	      cluster: q.cluster,
   110	      blocks: raw,
   111	      input,
   112	      medianYTimes2: Object.fromEntries(ARMS_FE.map((a) => [a, med(raw.filter((b) => b.outcomes[a]).map((b) => b.outcomes[a].y))])),
   113	      survivedByArm: Object.fromEntries(ARMS_FE.map((a) => [a, { reps: raw.reduce((t, b) => t + (b.outcomes[a]?.survivedReps ?? 0), 0), of: raw.filter((b) => b.outcomes[a]).length * p.R }])),
   114	      survivalPositiveBlocks: raw.filter((b) => (b.contrasts?.survivalVsInoc2 ?? 0) > 0).length,
   115	      fluxAttribution: valid === 0 ? { available: false, reason: "no valid block produced a measurement", growthAttributedBlocks: 0, coCulture: "unavailable" } : sat === 0 ? { available: true, reason: "no role counter reached saturation in any replicate; the co-culture arm (a) is never attributed", growthAttributedBlocks: raw.filter((b) => (b.outcomes.b?.growthAttributedReps ?? 0) * 2 >= p.R).length, coCulture: "unavailable" } : { available: false, reason: `a role counter reached ${p.roleSaturation} in ${sat} block(s)`, growthAttributedBlocks: 0, coCulture: "unavailable" },
   116	      aucMedianTimes2: Object.fromEntries(ARMS_FE.map((a) => [a, med(raw.filter((b) => b.outcomes[a]).map((b) => b.outcomes[a].auc))])),
   117	    });
   118	  }
   119	  const results = decideCandidatesFe(inputs, p.viableBlocksMin);
   120	  const caveats = [p.powerStatement];
   121	  if (candidates.length === 0) caveats.push("No candidate qualified on the dev positive control, so no fresh seed was simulated: this is a statement about the sensitivity of the assay at realistic polymer doses, not about niche construction.");
   122	  return { purpose: "confirmation", seeds: p.freshSeeds, ran: candidates.length > 0, candidates, inputs, results, panel: panelVerdictFe(results), caveats };
   123	}
   124	
   125	export interface FixturesReportFe {
   126	  protocol: { grid: number; kernelRadius: number; T2: number; R: number; reserve: { B: number; E: number }; delta: number };
   127	  seeds: readonly number[];
   128	  reports: FixtureReportFe[];
   129	  freeGap: FreeGapSeed[];
   130	  guards: GuardFe[];
   131	  /** Rung-1 W2 wall (allowP) at the frozen scale through the second-rung arms; diagnostic, never qualifying. */
   132	  handWallGate: FixtureReportFe;
   133	}
   134	
   135	export function fixturesReportFe(p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): FixturesReportFe {
   136	  const fp = fixtureProtocolFe(p);
   137	  const seeds = p.fixtureSeeds;
   138	  const delta = Math.ceil((fp.reserve.B * fp.R) / 50);
   139	  const reports: FixtureReportFe[] = [];
   140	  for (const v of ["far-field", "uniform", "footprint"] as FieldVariant[]) reports.push(decideFixtureFe(v === "far-field" ? "far-field-energy" : `energy-${v}`, seeds, seeds.map((s) => fieldBlock(v, s, fp)), delta));
   141	  reports.push(decideFixtureFe("takeover-only", seeds, seeds.map((s) => takeoverBlockFe(s, fp)), delta));
   142	  reports.push(decideFixtureFe("null-field", seeds, seeds.map((s) => nullBlockFe(s, fp)), delta));
   143	  const wp: ProtocolFe = { ...p, R: p.fixtureR };
   144	  const handWallGate = decideFixtureFe("hand-wall-gate-W2", seeds, seeds.map((s) => wallGateBlockFe2(s, wp)), Math.ceil((p.reserve.B * p.fixtureR) / 50));
   145	  return { protocol: { grid: fp.grid, kernelRadius: fp.kernelRadius, T2: fp.T2, R: fp.R, reserve: fp.reserve, delta }, seeds, reports, freeGap: seeds.map((s) => freeGapSeed(s, fp)), guards: runGuardsFe(fp), handWallGate };
   146	}
   147	
   148	export function stepProjectionFe(p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): { formula: string; freshBlocks: number; stepsPerBlock: number; devBlocks: number } {
   149	  const perBlock = 2 * p.T1 + 13 * p.R * p.T2;
   150	  return { formula: "per block 2*T1 + 13 arms * R * T2; dev blocks, replicate-noise, wall ladder (12 arms * R * T2 per wall seed per dose) and fixtures are calibration or unit scale", freshBlocks: p.panel.length * p.freshSeeds.length, stepsPerBlock: perBlock, devBlocks: p.panel.length * p.devSeeds.length };
   151	}

   172	  "Free energy cannot be matched together with total energy at equal matter: fU, fS and fB match free E exactly and carry a signed, evidence-only constructionGap in total energy. They sit on the weaker side of total energy, so they screen free-energy alone, not bound inventories.",
   173	  "The positive control is a hand-built polymer ring under ordinary placement (W1). It shows the assay can detect a polymer structure of that dose, not that a builder's field can pass. The polymer floor under the receiver (W2) is outside ordinary placement and is reported unavailable.",
   174	  "Sixteen fresh blocks with exact sign tests can only certify a benefit that is positive in about nine of ten blocks or more and clears delta on four contrasts and viability.    175	  "Summing R replicates reduces assay-seed noise, not block-to-block field variance; the block count carries that.",

   182	export function amendmentsFe(dev: DevReportFe, p: ProtocolFe): string[] {
   183	  const d = dev.candidates.map((c) => `cluster ${c.cluster}: delta ${p.delta[String(c.cluster)]}, ceiling ${c.builderPMedianTimes2}`).join("; ");
   184	  return [`Amendment 3 (2026-11-02, PLAN section 10): R = ${p.R}; doses ${p.doses.join(", ")}; ${d}; block, replicate, noise, wall and fixture seeds as enumerated in the protocol (${enumerateSeedsFe(p).length} seeds); frozen and pushed before any fresh seed was read. Rung 1 (docs/niche-construction-2026-11-01) is superseded only by a rung-2 label, not rewritten.`];
   185	}

   187	export function sealPayloadFe(body: Omit<PayloadFe, "integritySha256">): PayloadFe {
   188	  return { ...body, integritySha256: sha256HexJS(new TextEncoder().encode(canonicalJSON(body))) };
   189	}
   190	export function verifySealFe(p: PayloadFe): boolean {
   191	  const { integritySha256, ...body } = p;
   192	  return integritySha256 === sha256HexJS(new TextEncoder().encode(canonicalJSON(body)));
   193	}

   195	/** Build the sealed payload around a FROZEN dev report (calibration.json): only the fixtures and the fresh blocks are simulated here. */
   196	export function buildPayloadFe(registryDigest: string, dev: DevReportFe, p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE, log: (s: string) => void = () => {}): PayloadFe {
   197	  const fresh = freshReportFe(dev, p, log);
   198	  return sealPayloadFe({ kind: "niche-fe-evidence", version: EVIDENCE_VERSION_FE, protocol: p, protocolHash: protocolHashFe(registryDigest, p), registryDigest, seedCount: enumerateSeedsFe(p).length, stepProjection: stepProjectionFe(p), dev, fresh, fixtures: fixturesReportFe(p), powerStatement: p.powerStatement, limits: [...LIMITS_FE], amendments: amendmentsFe(dev, p) });
   199	}

     1	# I06b evidence: free-energy-matched comparators, second rung (2026-11-02)
     2	
     3	Machine-readable: `evidence.json` (sealed, seal 42ba2c6b3e935f32...), `manifest.json`, `calibration.json`, `protocol.lock.json`. Logs: `calibrate.log`, `write.log`, `checks.log`, `rung1-replay.log`. Freeze commit e7a48c06, pushed to `origin/claude/i06-free-energy-matched` before `--write` (the generator refuses otherwise). Exploratory, CPU only, mutation off.
     4	
     5	## Result
     6	
     7	**unsupported-measurement for all three candidates (clusters 0, 4, 9). Not a negative.** The ordinary-placement positive control found no detectable wall at any tested dose, so by the pre-stated rule no fresh builder block was simulated (fresh seeds 8601501-516 never read). This is the stated reason "none exists": no ordinary-placement wall geometry is detectable at realistic scale in this assay.
     8	
     9	- Placement: W2 (polymer floor under the receiver) is unavailable (0 common placeable cells); W1 (ring) is the only legal geometry.
    10	- W1 dose ladder (8 wall seeds, four gating contrasts incl. inoc2, R = 4): 60, 120, 240 never reach 7 of 8 on all contrasts (best weakest-contrast median +1.5 at 240, above every ceiling); 600 and 1200 are worse than the comparators (medians about -172 and -190). Ceilings (2 x builder polymer): 200, 204, 169.
    11	- Noise floor delta (summed R = 4): 1152, 1031, 1169. Divided by R that is 288, 258, 292 against rung 1's 341, 291, 404 (floor lowered 15.5%, 11.4%, 27.7%): not enough, and the wall effect is far below even the new floor (G-NOISE needs the weakest median at least 2 x delta).
    12	- Comparators: fU, fS, fB match the field arm's free energy exactly (9,394 for cluster 0 seed 8601001, against receiver-alone's 19,864 at equal total 25,624) with exact zero ledger residuals; they hold less total energy (constructionGap +10,470, +10,470, +10,380). `inoc2` negative in 24 of 24 dev blocks and equal to `inoc` (its max keeps d1E/d1B); matched-only contrast `inocMatched` was zero or negative in 21 and positive in 3 (reported, not gating).
    13	- far-field-energy fixture (8 seeds): naive 8/8 benefit; rung-1 inoculum contrast 8/8 positive (fooled); free-energy-matched `inoc2` 0/8 (caught); full gate rejects (poly 0/8). The claim is about the inoculum contrast only. Hand-wall W2 (allowP, frozen scale, diagnostic) passes the full gate: supported (7/8 on builder, poly, inoc2; 8/8 space), so the gate can say supported for a floor of 1200 polymer, which ordinary placement forbids.
    14	
    15	## What this does and does not say
    16	
    17	The assay at this scale cannot see a polymer structure of builder-like dose, so it cannot test whether a builder's field helps a receiver; it says nothing against niche construction. Next rung candidates (not run): a placement-legal control with real effect at small dose (e.g. protective polymer on cells the receiver may occupy via a different transfer rule), or a lower-variance readout than receiver biomass at T2.
    18

     1	/**
     2	 * I06b niche construction, second rung: free-energy-matched comparators, replicated readout, ordinary-placement dose-ladder positive control.
     3	 * Invoked through the first rung's CLI so there is one tool: `node tools/niche/niche.ts --rung fe <mode>`:
     4	 *   --bench          one block's step rate and the projected fresh run (informational)
     5	 *   --calibrate      dev blocks, replicate-noise control and the wall dose ladder only: writes calibration.json
     6	 *   --freeze         write protocol.lock.json (protocol hash, seeds, registry digest, calibration and source hashes) BEFORE any fresh run
     7	 *   --write          fixtures and fresh blocks of qualified candidates (calibration is loaded frozen); needs a verifying lock and a pushed identical freeze
     8	 *   --check          validate the saved artifacts without simulating
     9	 *   --replay-check   regenerate the payload from the frozen calibration and require byte equality (same freeze guard)

    43	const libFe = async () => {
    44	  (await helpers()).registerAliases();

    55	/** Digest over this rung's own reservation line(s) only: another slice's reservation never invalidates this evidence. */
    56	export const registryDigestFe = async (root = REPO): Promise<string> => {
    57	  const text = readFileSync(`${root}${REGISTRY_REL}`, "utf8");
    58	  const own = text.split("\n").filter((line) => line.includes(`"owner": "${OWNER}`));
    59	  if (own.length !== 1) throw new Error(`expected exactly one registry reservation owned by "${OWNER}", found ${own.length}`);
    60	  return sha256(own.join("\n"));
    61	};

    63	/** Every file whose bytes feed this rung's evidence: the first rung's source set plus this tool. */
    64	export const frozenSources = async (root = REPO): Promise<string[]> => [...(await helpers()).sourceFiles(root), "tools/niche/fe.ts"];
    65	export const hashSources = async (root = REPO): Promise<Record<string, string>> => {
    66	  const out: Record<string, string> = {};
    67	  for (const f of await frozenSources(root)) out[f] = await sha256(readFileSync(root + f));
    68	  return out;
    69	};

    71	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    72	// Freeze-before-run guard
    73	
    74	export type Git = (args: string[]) => string;
    75	export const realGit: Git = (args) => execFileSync("git", args, { cwd: REPO, encoding: "utf8", maxBuffer: 1 << 26 });
    76	
    77	/** Paths whose bytes the lock binds (or that the lock itself is): they must be committed and identical on the pushed branch. */
    78	export const PLAN_FE = `${OUT_DIR_FE}PLAN.md`;
    79	export const frozenPaths = async (root = REPO): Promise<string[]> => [...(await frozenSources(root)), REGISTRY_REL, rel(CALIBRATION_FE), rel(LOCK_FE), rel(PLAN_FE)];
    80	
    81	/**
    82	 * Refuse unless the freeze is clean and pushed: the frozen paths have no uncommitted change, and each path's blob on
    83	 * origin/<BRANCH> equals the blob in the working tree (git ls-tree against git hash-object). The lock cannot name its own commit,
    84	 * so this identical-on-the-pushed-branch check is the ordering proof. Returns problems (empty when the guard allows).
    85	 */
    86	export function freezeGuard(paths: string[], git: Git = realGit, branch = BRANCH): string[] {
    87	  const problems: string[] = [];
    88	  // The remote itself is asked (not a possibly stale remote-tracking ref); its commit must be present locally so its tree can be read.
    89	  let ref = "";
    90	  try {
    91	    ref = git(["ls-remote", "--exit-code", "origin", `refs/heads/${branch}`]).split(/\s+/)[0] ?? "";
    92	  } catch {
    93	    return [`origin has no refs/heads/${branch} (or cannot be reached): push the freeze commit first (git push -u origin ${branch})`];
    94	  }
    95	  if (!/^[0-9a-f]{40}$/.test(ref)) return [`could not read the commit of origin refs/heads/${branch}`];
    96	  let status = "";
    97	  try {
    98	    status = git(["status", "--porcelain", "--", ...paths]);
    99	  } catch (e) {
   100	    return [`git status failed: ${(e as Error).message}`];
   101	  }
   102	  for (const line of status.split("\n").filter(Boolean)) problems.push(`frozen file not committed clean: ${line.trim()}`);
   103	  let tree = "";
   104	  try {
   105	    tree = git(["ls-tree", "-r", ref, "--", ...paths]);
   106	  } catch {
   107	    return [...problems, `commit ${ref} of origin/${branch} is not in the local object store: git fetch origin ${branch}`];
   108	  }
   109	  const remote = new Map<string, string>();
   110	  for (const line of tree.split("\n").filter(Boolean)) {
   111	    const m = /^\d+ blob ([0-9a-f]+)\t(.+)$/.exec(line);
   112	    if (m) remote.set(m[2], m[1]);
   113	  }
   114	  const local = git(["hash-object", "--", ...paths]).split("\n").filter(Boolean);
   115	  paths.forEach((path, i) => {
   116	    if (!remote.has(path)) problems.push(`${path} is not on ${ref}`);
   117	    else if (remote.get(path) !== local[i]) problems.push(`${path} differs from ${ref}`);
   118	  });
   119	  return problems;
   120	}
   121	
   122	/** Lock verification: protocol, calibration, seeds, registry digest and every source hash against the current tree. */
   123	export async function verifyLock(planPath = PLAN_FE): Promise<string[]> {
   124	  const L = await libFe();
   125	  const problems: string[] = [];
   126	  if (!existsSync(LOCK_FE)) return ["no protocol.lock.json: run --calibrate and --freeze before the fresh run"];
   127	  if (!existsSync(CALIBRATION_FE)) return ["no calibration.json"];
   128	  const lock = JSON.parse(readFileSync(LOCK_FE, "utf8"));
   129	  const digest = await registryDigestFe();
   130	  const canon = L.schema.canonicalJSON;
   131	  if (lock.registryDigest !== digest) problems.push("registry reservation changed since the freeze");
   132	  if (lock.protocolHash !== L.protocolHashFe(digest, L.PROTOCOL_FE)) problems.push("the protocol in code differs from protocol.lock.json");
   133	  if (canon(lock.seeds) !== canon(L.enumerateSeedsFe(L.PROTOCOL_FE))) problems.push("lock seed list differs from the protocol");
   134	  if (lock.calibrationSha256 !== (await sha256(readFileSync(CALIBRATION_FE)))) problems.push("calibration.json changed since the freeze");
   135	  if (lock.planSha256 !== (await sha256(readFileSync(planPath)))) problems.push("PLAN.md (with Amendment 3) changed since the freeze");
   136	  const now = await hashSources();
   137	  if (canon(lock.sources) !== canon(now)) for (const f of new Set([...Object.keys(lock.sources ?? {}), ...Object.keys(now)])) if (lock.sources?.[f] !== now[f]) problems.push(`source ${f} differs from the freeze`);
   138	  return problems;
   139	}
   140	
   141	export async function guarded(git: Git = realGit): Promise<void> {
   142	  const problems = [...(await verifyLock()), ...freezeGuard(await frozenPaths(), git)];
   143	  if (problems.length) throw new Error(`refusing to read any fresh seed:\n${problems.join("\n")}`);
   144	}

   147	
   148	const logger = (t0: number) => (s: string) => console.log(`[${Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000)}s] ${s}`);
   149	
   150	/** Every path that can simulate fresh seeds starts here, so the guard lives here: no lock, dirty tree or unpushed freeze means no simulation. */
   151	export async function generateFe(git: Git = realGit): Promise<unknown> {
   152	  await guarded(git);
   153	  const L = await libFe();
   154	  const dev = JSON.parse(readFileSync(CALIBRATION_FE, "utf8"));
   155	  return L.buildPayloadFe(await registryDigestFe(), dev, L.PROTOCOL_FE, logger(Date.now()));
   156	}

   158	export async function makeManifestFe(payload: any, evidenceText: string, seconds: number): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
   159	  return {
   160	    kind: "niche-fe-evidence-manifest",
   161	    payloadFileSha256: await sha256(evidenceText),
   162	    integritySha256: payload.integritySha256,
   163	    protocolHash: payload.protocolHash,
   164	    registryDigest: payload.registryDigest,
   165	    stepProjection: payload.stepProjection,
   166	    sources: await hashSources(),
   167	    provenance: { note: "not replayed; excluded from the deterministic payload", node: process.version, platform: `${platform()}-${arch()}`, regenerationSeconds: Math.round(seconds), generatedAt: new Date().toISOString() },
   168	  };
   169	}
   170	
   171	/** Validate saved artifacts without simulating. Returns problems (empty when sound). */
   172	export async function checkSavedFe(root = REPO): Promise<string[]> {
   173	  const L = await libFe();
   174	  const problems: string[] = [];
   175	  const paths = { evidence: EVIDENCE_FE, manifest: MANIFEST_FE, calibration: CALIBRATION_FE, lock: LOCK_FE };
   176	  for (const f of Object.values(paths)) if (!existsSync(f)) problems.push(`${f} is missing`);
   177	  if (problems.length) return problems;
   178	  const text = readFileSync(paths.evidence, "utf8");
   179	  const payload = JSON.parse(text);
   180	  const manifest = JSON.parse(readFileSync(paths.manifest, "utf8"));
   181	  const lock = JSON.parse(readFileSync(paths.lock, "utf8"));
   182	  const calibration = JSON.parse(readFileSync(paths.calibration, "utf8"));
   183	  const canon = L.schema.canonicalJSON;
   184	  if (!L.verifySealFe(payload)) problems.push("integritySha256 does not match the payload");
   185	  if (manifest.payloadFileSha256 !== (await sha256(text))) problems.push("manifest payloadFileSha256 does not match evidence.json");
   186	  if (manifest.integritySha256 !== payload.integritySha256) problems.push("manifest seal differs from the payload seal");
   187	  if (manifest.protocolHash !== payload.protocolHash) problems.push("manifest protocolHash differs from the payload");
   188	  const digest = await registryDigestFe(root);
   189	  if (payload.registryDigest !== digest) problems.push("this rung's registry reservation changed since the evidence was written");
   190	  if (lock.protocolHash !== payload.protocolHash) problems.push("protocol.lock.json does not match the payload protocol hash");
   191	  if (lock.protocolHash !== L.protocolHashFe(digest, L.PROTOCOL_FE)) problems.push("the protocol in code differs from the frozen lock");
   192	  if (canon(payload.protocol) !== canon(L.PROTOCOL_FE)) problems.push("payload protocol differs from the protocol in code");
   193	  if (canon(lock.seeds) !== canon(L.enumerateSeedsFe(L.PROTOCOL_FE))) problems.push("lock seed list differs from the protocol");
   194	  if (canon(calibration) !== canon(payload.dev)) problems.push("calibration.json differs from the dev section of evidence.json");
   195	  if (lock.calibrationSha256 !== (await sha256(readFileSync(paths.calibration)))) problems.push("lock calibrationSha256 does not match calibration.json");
   196	  for (const c of payload.dev.candidates) if (L.PROTOCOL_FE.delta[String(c.cluster)] !== c.deltaProposed) problems.push(`delta for cluster ${c.cluster} is not the dev replicate-noise floor`);
   197	  // Seed hygiene: every seed that can run lies in this rung's reservation, never in another owner's (rung 1's seeds included).
   198	  const registry = JSON.parse(readFileSync(`${root}${REGISTRY_REL}`, "utf8")).reservations as { first: number; last: number; owner: string }[];
   199	  const all = L.enumerateSeedsFe(L.PROTOCOL_FE).map((seed, i) => ({ id: `s${i}`, seed }));
   200	  for (const f of L.schema.seedCollisions(all, registry.filter((r) => !r.owner.startsWith(OWNER)), OWNER)) problems.push(`seed collision: ${f}`);
   201	  for (const s of all) if (!registry.some((r) => r.owner.startsWith(OWNER) && s.seed >= r.first && s.seed <= r.last)) problems.push(`seed ${s.seed} is outside this rung's reservation`);
   202	  // Decision and qualification recomputation from the stored inputs.
   203	  const recomputed = L.decideCandidatesFe(payload.fresh.inputs, L.PROTOCOL_FE.viableBlocksMin);
   204	  if (canon(recomputed) !== canon(payload.fresh.results)) problems.push("fresh results do not follow the stated decision rules");
   205	  if (canon(L.panelVerdictFe(recomputed)) !== canon(payload.fresh.panel)) problems.push("panel verdict does not follow the stated rules");
   206	  const qual = payload.dev.candidates.map((c: any) => L.qualify(c.cluster, c.deltaProposed, c.builderPMedianTimes2, payload.dev.ladder.rows, L.PROTOCOL_FE));
   207	  if (canon(qual) !== canon(payload.dev.qualification)) problems.push("qualification does not follow the stated positive-control rules");
   208	  for (const q of payload.dev.qualification) {
   209	    const sim = payload.fresh.candidates.some((c: any) => c.cluster === q.cluster);
   210	    if (sim !== q.qualified) problems.push(`cluster ${q.cluster}: ${q.qualified ? "qualified but not simulated" : "not qualified but simulated"}`);
   211	  }
   212	  // Every ledger exactly zero.
   213	  const blocks = [...payload.dev.candidates, ...payload.fresh.candidates].flatMap((c: any) => c.blocks);
   214	  for (const b of blocks) for (const [arm, recs] of Object.entries<any[]>(b.transfers)) for (const r of recs) if (r.residual !== "0" || r.matterResidual !== "0") problems.push(`block ${b.seed} arm ${arm} op ${r.op}: nonzero residual`);
   215	  // Sources.
   216	  const now = await hashSources(root);
   217	  if (canon(manifest.sources) !== canon(now)) for (const f of new Set([...Object.keys(manifest.sources), ...Object.keys(now)])) if (manifest.sources[f] !== now[f]) problems.push(`source ${f} changed since the evidence was written`);
   218	  if (canon(lock.sources) !== canon(now)) problems.push("sources differ from the frozen lock");
   219	  return problems;
   220	}
   221	
   222	export async function replayCheckFe(): Promise<string[]> {
   223	  const fresh = serialize(await generateFe());
   224	  return fresh === readFileSync(EVIDENCE_FE, "utf8") ? [] : ["regenerated payload differs from evidence.json"];
   225	}

   227	async function repinRung1(): Promise<void> {
   228	  const m = JSON.parse(readFileSync(RUNG1_MANIFEST, "utf8"));
   229	  const sources: Record<string, string> = {};
   230	  for (const f of (await helpers()).sourceFiles()) sources[f] = await sha256(readFileSync(REPO + f));
   231	  m.sources = sources;
   232	  writeAtomic(RUNG1_MANIFEST, serialize(m));
   233	  console.log(`re-pinned ${Object.keys(sources).length} source hashes in the first rung's manifest.json (nothing else changed)`);
   234	}
   235	
   236	export async function mainFe(argv: string[], injected?: Helpers): Promise<void> {
   237	  if (injected) helperModule = injected;
   238	  const mode = argv[2];
   239	  const L = await libFe();
   240	  const t0 = Date.now();
   241	  const log = logger(t0);
   242	  if (mode === "--bench") {
   243	    const p = L.PROTOCOL_FE;
   244	    const r = L.runBlockFe(p.panel[0], p.devSeeds[0], p);
   245	    const ms = Date.now() - t0;
   246	    const steps = 2 * p.T1 + 13 * p.R * p.T2;
   247	    console.log(`block valid=${r.valid}: ${steps} steps in ${ms} ms = ${(ms / steps).toFixed(2)} ms/step`);
   248	    const proj = L.stepProjectionFe(p);
   249	    console.log(`fresh run: ${proj.freshBlocks} blocks x ${proj.stepsPerBlock} steps ~ ${Math.round((proj.freshBlocks * ms) / 1000)} s; dev blocks ${proj.devBlocks} ~ ${Math.round((proj.devBlocks * ms) / 1000)} s`);
   250	    return;
   251	  }
   252	  if (mode === "--calibrate") {
   253	    const dev = L.devReportFe(L.PROTOCOL_FE, log);
   254	    writeAtomic(CALIBRATION_FE, serialize(dev));
   255	    for (const q of dev.qualification) console.log(`cluster ${q.cluster}: ${q.qualified ? "QUALIFIED" : "not qualified"} delta ${q.delta} ceiling ${q.ceiling} minDetectableDose ${q.minDetectableDose} qualifyingDose ${q.qualifyingDose}: ${q.reason}`);
   256	    console.log(`W2: ${dev.ladder.w2.status} (${dev.ladder.w2.reason})`);
   257	    console.log("wrote calibration.json (dev only). Copy the deltas into PROTOCOL_FE.delta, then --freeze.");
   258	    return;
   259	  }
   260	  if (mode === "--freeze") {
   261	    if (!existsSync(CALIBRATION_FE)) throw new Error("run --calibrate first");
   262	    if (existsSync(LOCK_FE) && !argv.includes("--force")) throw new Error("protocol.lock.json exists; pass --force to rewrite deliberately.");
   263	    const digest = await registryDigestFe();
   264	    const calibration = JSON.parse(readFileSync(CALIBRATION_FE, "utf8"));
   265	    for (const c of calibration.candidates) if (L.PROTOCOL_FE.delta[String(c.cluster)] !== c.deltaProposed) throw new Error(`PROTOCOL_FE.delta[${c.cluster}] must be ${c.deltaProposed} (the dev replicate-noise floor)`);
   266	    if (calibration.R !== L.PROTOCOL_FE.R) throw new Error("calibration was run with a different R");
   267	    const lock = { kind: "niche-fe-protocol-lock", protocolHash: L.protocolHashFe(digest, L.PROTOCOL_FE), registryDigest: digest, seeds: L.enumerateSeedsFe(L.PROTOCOL_FE), calibrationSha256: await sha256(readFileSync(CALIBRATION_FE)), planSha256: await sha256(readFileSync(PLAN_FE)), sources: await hashSources(), note: "written before the fresh-seed run; fresh results never tune the protocol", frozenAt: new Date().toISOString() };
   268	    writeAtomic(LOCK_FE, serialize(lock));
   269	    console.log(`wrote protocol.lock.json ${lock.protocolHash}`);
   270	    return;
   271	  }
   272	  if (mode === "--write") {
   273	    if (existsSync(EVIDENCE_FE) && !argv.includes("--force")) throw new Error("evidence.json exists; files are written once. Pass --force to rewrite deliberately.");
   274	    const payload = (await generateFe()) as any;
   275	    const seconds = (Date.now() - t0) / 1000;
   276	    const text = serialize(payload);
   277	    const manifestText = serialize(await makeManifestFe(payload, text, seconds));
   278	    writeFileSync(`${EVIDENCE_FE}.tmp`, text);
   279	    writeFileSync(`${MANIFEST_FE}.tmp`, manifestText);
   280	    renameSync(`${EVIDENCE_FE}.tmp`, EVIDENCE_FE);
   281	    renameSync(`${MANIFEST_FE}.tmp`, MANIFEST_FE);
   282	    console.log(`wrote evidence.json (${text.length} bytes) seal ${payload.integritySha256} in ${seconds.toFixed(0)} s; verdict: ${payload.fresh.panel.verdict}`);
   283	    return;
   284	  }
   285	  if (mode === "--check" || mode === "--replay-check") {
   286	    const problems = mode === "--check" ? await checkSavedFe() : await replayCheckFe();
   287	    if (problems.length) {
   288	      console.error(problems.join("\n"));
   289	      process.exit(1);
   290	    }
   291	    console.log(`rung fe ${mode} ok${mode === "--replay-check" ? ` (${Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000)} s)` : ""}`);
   292	    return;
   293	  }
   294	  if (mode === "--repin-rung1") return repinRung1();
   295	  console.error("usage: node tools/niche/niche.ts --rung fe --bench | --calibrate | --freeze | --write | --check | --replay-check | --repin-rung1");
   296	  process.exit(2);
   297	}

    44	  (await helpers()).registerAliases();
    45	  const [evidence, protocol, schema, decision] = await Promise.all([
    46	    import("../../packages/runner/src/niche/evidenceFe.ts"),
    47	    import("../../packages/runner/src/niche/protocol.ts"),
    48	    import("../../packages/schema/src/index.ts"),
    49	    import("../../packages/runner/src/niche/decisionFe.ts"),
    50	  ]);
    51	  const [replicate, dose] = await Promise.all([import("../../packages/runner/src/niche/replicate.ts"), import("../../packages/runner/src/niche/dose.ts")]);
    52	  return { ...evidence, ...protocol, ...decision, ...replicate, qualify: dose.qualify, schema };
    53	};
    54	
    55	/** Digest over this rung's own reservation line(s) only: another slice's reservation never invalidates this evidence. */
    56	export const registryDigestFe = async (root = REPO): Promise<string> => {
    57	  const text = readFileSync(`${root}${REGISTRY_REL}`, "utf8");
    58	  const own = text.split("\n").filter((line) => line.includes(`"owner": "${OWNER}`));
    59	  if (own.length !== 1) throw new Error(`expected exactly one registry reservation owned by "${OWNER}", found ${own.length}`);
    60	  return sha256(own.join("\n"));
    61	};
    62	
    63	/** Every file whose bytes feed this rung's evidence: the first rung's source set plus this tool. */
    64	export const frozenSources = async (root = REPO): Promise<string[]> => [...(await helpers()).sourceFiles(root), "tools/niche/fe.ts"];
    65	export const hashSources = async (root = REPO): Promise<Record<string, string>> => {
    66	  const out: Record<string, string> = {};
    67	  for (const f of await frozenSources(root)) out[f] = await sha256(readFileSync(root + f));
    68	  return out;
    69	};

    71	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    72	// Freeze-before-run guard
    73	
    74	export type Git = (args: string[]) => string;
    75	export const realGit: Git = (args) => execFileSync("git", args, { cwd: REPO, encoding: "utf8", maxBuffer: 1 << 26 });
    76	
    77	/** Paths whose bytes the lock binds (or that the lock itself is): they must be committed and identical on the pushed branch. */
    78	export const PLAN_FE = `${OUT_DIR_FE}PLAN.md`;
    79	export const frozenPaths = async (root = REPO): Promise<string[]> => [...(await frozenSources(root)), REGISTRY_REL, rel(CALIBRATION_FE), rel(LOCK_FE), rel(PLAN_FE)];
    80	
    81	/**
    82	 * Refuse unless the freeze is clean and pushed: the frozen paths have no uncommitted change, and each path's blob on
    83	 * origin/<BRANCH> equals the blob in the working tree (git ls-tree against git hash-object). The lock cannot name its own commit,
    84	 * so this identical-on-the-pushed-branch check is the ordering proof. Returns problems (empty when the guard allows).
    85	 */
    86	export function freezeGuard(paths: string[], git: Git = realGit, branch = BRANCH): string[] {
    87	  const problems: string[] = [];
    88	  // The remote itself is asked (not a possibly stale remote-tracking ref); its commit must be present locally so its tree can be read.
    89	  let ref = "";
    90	  try {
    91	    ref = git(["ls-remote", "--exit-code", "origin", `refs/heads/${branch}`]).split(/\s+/)[0] ?? "";
    92	  } catch {
    93	    return [`origin has no refs/heads/${branch} (or cannot be reached): push the freeze commit first (git push -u origin ${branch})`];
    94	  }
    95	  if (!/^[0-9a-f]{40}$/.test(ref)) return [`could not read the commit of origin refs/heads/${branch}`];
    96	  let status = "";
    97	  try {
    98	    status = git(["status", "--porcelain", "--", ...paths]);
    99	  } catch (e) {
   100	    return [`git status failed: ${(e as Error).message}`];
   101	  }
   102	  for (const line of status.split("\n").filter(Boolean)) problems.push(`frozen file not committed clean: ${line.trim()}`);
   103	  let tree = "";
   104	  try {
   105	    tree = git(["ls-tree", "-r", ref, "--", ...paths]);
   106	  } catch {
   107	    return [...problems, `commit ${ref} of origin/${branch} is not in the local object store: git fetch origin ${branch}`];
   108	  }
   109	  const remote = new Map<string, string>();
   110	  for (const line of tree.split("\n").filter(Boolean)) {
   111	    const m = /^\d+ blob ([0-9a-f]+)\t(.+)$/.exec(line);
   112	    if (m) remote.set(m[2], m[1]);
   113	  }
   114	  const local = git(["hash-object", "--", ...paths]).split("\n").filter(Boolean);
   115	  paths.forEach((path, i) => {
   116	    if (!remote.has(path)) problems.push(`${path} is not on ${ref}`);
   117	    else if (remote.get(path) !== local[i]) problems.push(`${path} differs from ${ref}`);
   118	  });
   119	  return problems;
   120	}
   121	
   122	/** Lock verification: protocol, calibration, seeds, registry digest and every source hash against the current tree. */
   123	export async function verifyLock(planPath = PLAN_FE): Promise<string[]> {
   124	  const L = await libFe();
   125	  const problems: string[] = [];
   126	  if (!existsSync(LOCK_FE)) return ["no protocol.lock.json: run --calibrate and --freeze before the fresh run"];
   127	  if (!existsSync(CALIBRATION_FE)) return ["no calibration.json"];
   128	  const lock = JSON.parse(readFileSync(LOCK_FE, "utf8"));
   129	  const digest = await registryDigestFe();
   130	  const canon = L.schema.canonicalJSON;
   131	  if (lock.registryDigest !== digest) problems.push("registry reservation changed since the freeze");
   132	  if (lock.protocolHash !== L.protocolHashFe(digest, L.PROTOCOL_FE)) problems.push("the protocol in code differs from protocol.lock.json");
   133	  if (canon(lock.seeds) !== canon(L.enumerateSeedsFe(L.PROTOCOL_FE))) problems.push("lock seed list differs from the protocol");
   134	  if (lock.calibrationSha256 !== (await sha256(readFileSync(CALIBRATION_FE)))) problems.push("calibration.json changed since the freeze");
   135	  if (lock.planSha256 !== (await sha256(readFileSync(planPath)))) problems.push("PLAN.md (with Amendment 3) changed since the freeze");
   136	  const now = await hashSources();
   137	  if (canon(lock.sources) !== canon(now)) for (const f of new Set([...Object.keys(lock.sources ?? {}), ...Object.keys(now)])) if (lock.sources?.[f] !== now[f]) problems.push(`source ${f} differs from the freeze`);
   138	  return problems;
   139	}
   140	
   141	export async function guarded(git: Git = realGit): Promise<void> {
   142	  const problems = [...(await verifyLock()), ...freezeGuard(await frozenPaths(), git)];
   143	  if (problems.length) throw new Error(`refusing to read any fresh seed:\n${problems.join("\n")}`);
   144	}

   148	const logger = (t0: number) => (s: string) => console.log(`[${Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000)}s] ${s}`);

   150	/** Every path that can simulate fresh seeds starts here, so the guard lives here: no lock, dirty tree or unpushed freeze means no simulation. */
   151	export async function generateFe(git: Git = realGit): Promise<unknown> {
   152	  await guarded(git);
   153	  const L = await libFe();
   154	  const dev = JSON.parse(readFileSync(CALIBRATION_FE, "utf8"));
   155	  return L.buildPayloadFe(await registryDigestFe(), dev, L.PROTOCOL_FE, logger(Date.now()));
   156	}

   159	  return {
   160	    kind: "niche-fe-evidence-manifest",

   162	    integritySha256: payload.integritySha256,
   163	    protocolHash: payload.protocolHash,
   164	    registryDigest: payload.registryDigest,
   165	    stepProjection: payload.stepProjection,
   166	    sources: await hashSources(),
   167	    provenance: { note: "not replayed; excluded from the deterministic payload", node: process.version, platform: `${platform()}-${arch()}`, regenerationSeconds: Math.round(seconds), generatedAt: new Date().toISOString() },

   185	  if (manifest.payloadFileSha256 !== (await sha256(text))) problems.push("manifest payloadFileSha256 does not match evidence.json");

     1	// I06b replicated Stage 2 (PLAN section 4.2): every arm's Stage 2 is run for R sim seeds (block seed + k * stride), the same
     2	// offsets in every arm, so injury steps and RNG keys coincide across arms. Stage 1 histories and transfers are shared.
     3	// Primary endpoint Y = integer sum over the R replicates of the receiver-labelled biomass at T2.
     4	import { cloneAudit, cloneState, NicheInvalid, snap, type Snap, type TransferRecord } from "./transfer.ts";
     5	import { buildArms, placeAll, type PreArm } from "./arms.ts";
     6	import { runArm, historyCost, type ArmOutcome, type Hooks, type HistoryCost } from "./assay.ts";
     7	import { buildFreeMatched, freeRows, type FreeRow } from "./freeEnergy.ts";
     8	import { PROTOCOL_FE, candidateOf, type ProtocolFe } from "./protocol.ts";

    10	export const ARMS_FE = ["a", "b", "c", "cP", "cNP", "nb", "ab", "d0", "d1E", "d1B", "fU", "fS", "fB"] as const;
    11	export type ArmIdFe = (typeof ARMS_FE)[number] | string;
    12	
    13	export interface ArmOutcomeFe {
    14	  id: string;
    15	  /** Receiver-labelled B at T2 per replicate, and their sum Y. */
    16	  ys: number[];
    17	  y: number;
    18	  /** Replicates in which the receiver B at T2 was above zero. */
    19	  survivedReps: number;
    20	  /** Secondary, never gating: area under the receiver-B census curve summed over replicates (each curve: sum over the censuses after the start). */
    21	  auc: number;
    22	  builderB: number;
    23	  synthesis: number | null;
    24	  saturated: boolean;
    25	  growthAttributedReps: number;
    26	  energyEnd: string;
    27	  endHashes: string[];
    28	}
    29	
    30	export const seedFor = (seed: number, k: number, p: { replicateStride: number } = PROTOCOL_FE): number => seed + k * p.replicateStride;
    31	
    32	/** Run the same placed arm for R replicate sim seeds; every census of every replicate is audited to an exact zero residual (runArm throws otherwise). */
    33	export function runArmReplicated(x: PreArm, seed: number, p: ProtocolFe, R: number = p.R, hooks: Hooks = {}, offsets?: readonly number[]): ArmOutcomeFe {
    34	  const ks = offsets ?? Array.from({ length: R }, (_, k) => k);
    35	  const outs: ArmOutcome[] = ks.map((k) => {
    36	    const rep: PreArm = { id: x.id, state: cloneState(x.state, { ...x.state.cfg, seed: seedFor(seed, k, p) }), audit: cloneAudit(x.audit), records: [] };
    37	    return runArm(rep, p, hooks);
    38	  });
    39	  const sum = (f: (o: ArmOutcome) => number) => outs.reduce((t, o) => t + f(o), 0);
    40	  return {
    41	    id: x.id,
    42	    ys: outs.map((o) => o.y),
    43	    y: sum((o) => o.y),
    44	    survivedReps: outs.filter((o) => o.survived).length,
    45	    auc: sum((o) => o.receiverB.slice(1).reduce((t, v) => t + v, 0)),
    46	    builderB: sum((o) => o.builderB),
    47	    synthesis: x.id === "a" ? null : sum((o) => o.synthesis ?? 0),
    48	    saturated: outs.some((o) => o.saturated),
    49	    growthAttributedReps: outs.filter((o) => o.growthAttributed).length,
    50	    energyEnd: outs[0].energyEnd,
    51	    endHashes: outs.map((o) => o.endHash),
    52	  };
    53	}

    55	export interface ContrastsFe {
    56	  builder: number;
    57	  poly: number;
    58	  space: number;
    59	  /** Free-energy-matched: Y(b) - max over every constructed comparator (d1E, d1B, fU, fS, fB). */
    60	  inoc2: number;
    61	  /** First rung's contrast, reported next to inoc2 (summed-R units). */
    62	  inoc: number;
    63	  /** Reported, never gating: Y(b) minus the best of the three free-energy-matched comparators alone (fU, fS, fB), so the matching is read without the first rung's comparators in the max. */
    64	  inocMatched: number;
    65	  spaceP: number;
    66	  spaceNP: number;
    67	  naive: number;
    68	  /** Paired survival (replicates with receiver present): b minus the best comparator. Attribution only. */
    69	  survivalVsInoc2: number;
    70	}
    71	
    72	export function contrastsOfFe(o: Partial<Record<string, ArmOutcomeFe>>): ContrastsFe {
    73	  const g = (id: string): ArmOutcomeFe => {
    74	    const v = o[id];
    75	    if (!v) throw new NicheInvalid(`arm ${id} missing`);
    76	    return v;
    77	  };
    78	  const yb = g("b").y;
    79	  const comps = ["d1E", "d1B", "fU", "fS", "fB"].filter((id) => o[id]);
    80	  return {
    81	    builder: yb - g("nb").y,
    82	    poly: yb - g("ab").y,
    83	    space: yb - g("c").y,
    84	    inoc2: yb - Math.max(...comps.map((id) => g(id).y)),
    85	    inoc: yb - Math.max(g("d1E").y, g("d1B").y),
    86	    inocMatched: yb - Math.max(...["fU", "fS", "fB"].filter((id) => o[id]).map((id) => g(id).y)),
    87	    spaceP: yb - g("cP").y,
    88	    spaceNP: yb - g("cNP").y,
    89	    naive: yb - g("d0").y,
    90	    survivalVsInoc2: g("b").survivedReps - Math.max(...comps.map((id) => g(id).survivedReps)),
    91	  };
    92	}

    94	export interface TransferLite {
    95	  op: string;
    96	  energyBefore: string;
    97	  energyAfter: string;
    98	  residual: string;
    99	  matterResidual: string;
   100	  detail: Record<string, number | string>;
   101	}
   102	export const liteTransfers = (rs: readonly TransferRecord[]): TransferLite[] => rs.map((r) => ({ op: r.op, energyBefore: r.before.energy, energyAfter: r.after.energy, residual: r.residual, matterResidual: r.matterResidual, detail: r.detail }));

   104	export interface BlockResultFe {
   105	  seed: number;
   106	  cluster: number;
   107	  valid: boolean;
   108	  invalidReason: string | null;
   109	  nPlaced: number;
   110	  dE: number;
   111	  dB: number;
   112	  dBFree: number;
   113	  refused: { id: string; reason: string }[];
   114	  costs: HistoryCost[];
   115	  /** Free energy and total energy per arm, before and after the shared inoculum placement. */
   116	  freeBefore: FreeRow[];
   117	  freeAfter: FreeRow[];
   118	  startTotals: Record<string, Snap>;
   119	  transfers: Record<string, TransferLite[]>;
   120	  outcomes: Record<string, ArmOutcomeFe>;
   121	  contrasts: ContrastsFe | null;
   122	  /** Receiver survived (B > 0) in at least R - 1 of R replicates in the field arm b. */
   123	  viable: boolean;
   124	}
   125	
   126	/** One block: Stage 1 histories (shared), transfers, shared placement, free-energy-matched comparators, R replicates of every Stage 2 arm. An invalid block is reported, never dropped. */
   127	export function runBlockFe(cluster: number, seed: number, p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE, hooks: Hooks = {}): BlockResultFe {
   128	  const base: BlockResultFe = { seed, cluster, valid: false, invalidReason: null, nPlaced: 0, dE: 0, dB: 0, dBFree: 0, refused: [], costs: [], freeBefore: [], freeAfter: [], startTotals: {}, transfers: {}, outcomes: {}, contrasts: null, viable: false };
   129	  let arms: PreArm[] = [];
   130	  try {
   131	    const built = buildArms(candidateOf(cluster, p), seed, p);
   132	    arms = built.arms;
   133	    base.dE = built.dE;
   134	    base.dB = built.dB;
   135	    base.costs = [historyCost("builder", built.histories.builder.state, p), historyCost("nonbuilder", built.histories.nonbuilder.state, p)];
   136	    const fm = buildFreeMatched(arms.find((x) => x.id === "b")!, p);
   137	    base.refused = fm.refused;
   138	    base.dBFree = fm.dB;
   139	    if (fm.arms.length === 0) throw new NicheInvalid(`every free-energy-matched comparator was refused: ${fm.refused.map((r) => `${r.id} (${r.reason})`).join("; ")}`);
   140	    arms = [...arms, ...fm.arms];
   141	    base.freeBefore = freeRows(arms);
   142	    base.nPlaced = placeAll(arms, p).length;
   143	    base.freeAfter = freeRows(arms);
   144	    for (const x of arms) {
   145	      base.startTotals[x.id] = snap(x.state, x.audit);
   146	      base.transfers[x.id] = liteTransfers(x.records);
   147	    }
   148	    for (const x of arms) base.outcomes[x.id] = runArmReplicated(x, seed, p, p.R, hooks);
   149	    base.contrasts = contrastsOfFe(base.outcomes);
   150	    base.viable = base.outcomes.b.survivedReps >= p.R - 1;
   151	    base.valid = true;
   152	  } catch (e) {
   153	    if (!(e instanceof NicheInvalid)) throw e;
   154	    base.invalidReason = e.reason;
   155	    for (const x of arms) base.transfers[x.id] ??= liteTransfers(x.records);
   156	  }
   157	  return base;
   158	}
   159	
   160	/** Replicate-noise control for one block: the builder-removed (b) and nonbuilder-removed (nb) arms, Y over replicate set A = offsets 0..R-1 against a disjoint set B = offsets R..2R-1. */
   161	export function noiseBlock(cluster: number, seed: number, p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): { absBuilder: number; absNonbuilder: number } {
   162	  if (2 * p.R - 1 > p.maxOffsetIndex) throw new NicheInvalid(`R = ${p.R} needs offsets above the reserved ${p.maxOffsetIndex}; extend the registry reservation before escalating`);
   163	  const built = buildArms(candidateOf(cluster, p), seed, p);
   164	  placeAll(built.arms, p);
   165	  const A = Array.from({ length: p.R }, (_, k) => k), B = Array.from({ length: p.R }, (_, k) => k + p.R);
   166	  const y = (id: string, offs: number[]) => runArmReplicated(built.arms.find((x) => x.id === id)!, seed, p, p.R, {}, offs).y;
   167	  return { absBuilder: Math.abs(y("b", A) - y("b", B)), absNonbuilder: Math.abs(y("nb", A) - y("nb", B)) };
   168	}
     1	// I06b free-energy-matched comparators (PLAN section 4.1). Each comparator is a separately constructed receiver-alone lattice
     2	// whose free energy E (the lattice channel) equals that of the builder-removed arm `b`, with `b`'s matter and reserve. Its ledger
     3	// starts at its own constructed total and conserves exactly: no credit enters the audit. The comparison-budget difference
     4	// constructionGap = total(b) - total(f) is signed, a BigInt, and lives in evidence only.
     5	import { CH, MOT_ZERO, allocState, cellCount, totalsOf, validateState, type WorldState } from "@bl/schema";
     6	import { outsideRegion, regionCells, type PreArm } from "./arms.ts";
     7	import { PROTOCOL_FE, type ProtocolFe } from "./protocol.ts";
     8	import { NicheInvalid, assertAudit, debitEven, reserveEnergy, type Audit } from "./transfer.ts";
     9	
    10	export const FREE_IDS = ["fU", "fS", "fB"] as const;
    11	export type FreeId = (typeof FREE_IDS)[number];
    12	
    13	/** Sum of the lattice free-energy channel E. */
    14	export const latticeE = (s: WorldState): bigint => totalsOf(s.cfg, s.cells).E;
    15	/** Total energy of an arm: lattice (all channels at their chemical value) plus the off-lattice reserve. */
    16	export const totalEnergyOf = (s: WorldState, a: Audit): bigint => totalsOf(s.cfg, s.cells).energy + reserveEnergy(s.cfg, a);
    17	
    18	export interface FreeRow {
    19	  id: string;
    20	  /** Lattice free energy E, and total energy including the reserve, at the stated phase. */
    21	  freeE: string;
    22	  total: string;
    23	  /** total(b) - total(arm): gap > 0 means the comparator holds less total energy than b (weaker on total), gap < 0 more. */
    24	  constructionGap: string;
    25	  /** Free-energy difference to b (arm - b). */
    26	  freeGapToB: string;
    27	  flag: "comparator-excess" | "comparator-deficit" | "total-matched" | "reference";
    28	}
    29	
    30	export function freeRows(arms: readonly PreArm[]): FreeRow[] {
    31	  const b = arms.find((x) => x.id === "b");
    32	  if (!b) return [];
    33	  const bTotal = totalEnergyOf(b.state, b.audit), bFree = latticeE(b.state);
    34	  return arms.map((x) => {
    35	    const total = totalEnergyOf(x.state, x.audit), free = latticeE(x.state);
    36	    const gap = bTotal - total;
    37	    return { id: x.id, freeE: `${free}`, total: `${total}`, constructionGap: `${gap}`, freeGapToB: `${free - bFree}`, flag: x.id === "b" ? "reference" : gap < 0n ? "comparator-excess" : gap > 0n ? "comparator-deficit" : "total-matched" };
    38	  });
    39	}
    40	
    41	/** A receiver-alone lattice with b's matter spread uniformly as nutrient A and a caller-chosen E field; reserve as b's. Own audit baseline. */
    42	function freshLattice(b: PreArm, id: FreeId, efield: (n: number) => number[]): PreArm {
    43	  const cfg = b.state.cfg;
    44	  const n = cellCount(cfg);
    45	  const s = allocState(cfg);
    46	  const matter = totalsOf(cfg, b.state.cells).matter;
    47	  const base = matter / BigInt(n), rem = Number(matter % BigInt(n));
    48	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) s.cells[CH.A * n + i] = Number(base) + (i < rem ? 1 : 0);
    49	  const e = efield(n);
    50	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) s.cells[CH.E * n + i] = e[i];
    51	  s.cells.fill(MOT_ZERO, CH.MOT * n, (CH.MOT + 1) * n);
    52	  s.step = b.state.step;
    53	  const audit: Audit = { energyStart: 0n, matterStart: 0n, reserveB: b.audit.reserveB, reserveE: b.audit.reserveE, heatAnalytic: 0n };
    54	  return { id, state: s, audit, records: [] };
    55	}
    56	
    57	/** Own baseline: the constructed start (lattice plus reserve, no analytic heat, lightIn and heatOut zero). */
    58	function openOwnBaseline(x: PreArm): void {
    59	  const t = totalsOf(x.state.cfg, x.state.cells);
    60	  x.audit.energyStart = t.energy + reserveEnergy(x.state.cfg, x.audit);
    61	  x.audit.matterStart = t.matter + BigInt(x.audit.reserveB);
    62	  assertAudit(x.state, x.audit, `${x.id} start`);
    63	  const errs = validateState(x.state);
    64	  if (errs.length) throw new NicheInvalid(`credit-overdraw: ${x.id} fails validateState: ${errs[0]}`);
    65	}
    66	
    67	export const uniformSplit = (total: bigint, n: number): number[] => {
    68	  const base = total / BigInt(n), rem = Number(total % BigInt(n));
    69	  return Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => Number(base) + (i < rem ? 1 : 0));
    70	};
    71	
    72	/**
    73	 * fB: fS plus the biomass the field held in the footprint-plus-halo region (the first rung's d1B quantity), handed over in the reserve
    74	 * as bound matter taken from the lattice outside the region. NO lattice energy debit, so free E stays equal to b cell for cell
    75	 * before and after placement. Relative to fS, fB holds (eB - eA) * dB more total energy; its own gap to b is recorded with its
    76	 * sign (it can be positive: a field that binds energy in polymer or waste still holds more than fB; negative or zero otherwise).
    77	 */
    78	export function fundBiomass(f: PreArm, dB: number, outside: readonly number[]): void {
    79	  try {
    80	    debitEven(f.state, CH.A, dB, outside);
    81	  } catch (e) {
    82	    if (e instanceof NicheInvalid) throw new NicheInvalid(`credit-overdraw: matched biomass ${dB} cannot be funded from the lattice outside the region (${e.reason})`);
    83	    throw e;
    84	  }
    85	  f.audit.reserveB += dB;
    86	}
    87	
    88	export interface FreeMatched {
    89	  arms: PreArm[];
    90	  /** Comparators that could not be built (refused, never clamped): id and reason. */
    91	  refused: { id: FreeId; reason: string }[];
    92	  dB: number;
    93	}
    94	
    95	/** Build fU, fS and fB around the conditioned builder-removed arm `b` (before placement). A refused one is reported, not dropped silently. */
    96	export function buildFreeMatched(b: PreArm, p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): FreeMatched {
    97	  const arms: PreArm[] = [];
    98	  const refused: FreeMatched["refused"] = [];
    99	  const n = cellCount(b.state.cfg);
   100	  const bE = latticeE(b.state);
   101	  const copyE = (): number[] => Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => b.state.cells[CH.E * n + i]);
   102	  const region = regionCells(p), outside = outsideRegion(p);
   103	  const sumB = (cells: readonly number[]) => cells.reduce((t, i) => t + b.state.cells[CH.B * n + i], 0);
   104	  const dB = sumB(region);
   105	  const attempt = (id: FreeId, make: () => PreArm): void => {
   106	    try {
   107	      const f = make();
   108	      openOwnBaseline(f);
   109	      arms.push(f);
   110	    } catch (e) {
   111	      if (!(e instanceof NicheInvalid)) throw e;
   112	      refused.push({ id, reason: e.reason });
   113	    }
   114	  };
   115	  attempt("fU", () => freshLattice(b, "fU", () => uniformSplit(bE, n)));
   116	  attempt("fS", () => freshLattice(b, "fS", copyE));
   117	  attempt("fB", () => {
   118	    const f = freshLattice(b, "fB", copyE);
   119	    fundBiomass(f, dB, outside);
   120	    return f;
   121	  });
   122	  return { arms, refused, dB };
   123	}

    21	    // d0 (and so d1E, d1B) hold less free energy than the field; fU, fS, fB hold the same.

    29	      const r = freeGapSeed(s, fp);
