   236	export async function mainFe(argv: string[], injected?: Helpers): Promise<void> {
   237	  if (injected) helperModule = injected;
   238	  const mode = argv[2];
   239	  const L = await libFe();
   240	  const t0 = Date.now();
   241	  const log = logger(t0);
   242	  if (mode === "--bench") {
   243	    const p = L.PROTOCOL_FE;
   244	    const r = L.runBlockFe(p.panel[0], p.devSeeds[0], p);
   245	    const ms = Date.now() - t0;
   246	    const steps = 2 * p.T1 + 13 * p.R * p.T2;
   247	    console.log(`block valid=${r.valid}: ${steps} steps in ${ms} ms = ${(ms / steps).toFixed(2)} ms/step`);
   248	    const proj = L.stepProjectionFe(p);
   249	    console.log(`fresh run: ${proj.freshBlocks} blocks x ${proj.stepsPerBlock} steps ~ ${Math.round((proj.freshBlocks * ms) / 1000)} s; dev blocks ${proj.devBlocks} ~ ${Math.round((proj.devBlocks * ms) / 1000)} s`);
   250	    return;
   251	  }
   252	  if (mode === "--calibrate") {
   253	    const dev = L.devReportFe(L.PROTOCOL_FE, log);
   254	    writeAtomic(CALIBRATION_FE, serialize(dev));
   255	    for (const q of dev.qualification) console.log(`cluster ${q.cluster}: ${q.qualified ? "QUALIFIED" : "not qualified"} delta ${q.delta} ceiling ${q.ceiling} minDetectableDose ${q.minDetectableDose} qualifyingDose ${q.qualifyingDose}: ${q.reason}`);
   256	    console.log(`W2: ${dev.ladder.w2.status} (${dev.ladder.w2.reason})`);
   257	    console.log("wrote calibration.json (dev only). Copy the deltas into PROTOCOL_FE.delta, then --freeze.");
   258	    return;
   259	  }
   260	  if (mode === "--freeze") {
   261	    if (!existsSync(CALIBRATION_FE)) throw new Error("run --calibrate first");
   262	    if (existsSync(LOCK_FE) && !argv.includes("--force")) throw new Error("protocol.lock.json exists; pass --force to rewrite deliberately.");
   263	    const digest = await registryDigestFe();
   264	    const calibration = JSON.parse(readFileSync(CALIBRATION_FE, "utf8"));
   265	    for (const c of calibration.candidates) if (L.PROTOCOL_FE.delta[String(c.cluster)] !== c.deltaProposed) throw new Error(`PROTOCOL_FE.delta[${c.cluster}] must be ${c.deltaProposed} (the dev replicate-noise floor)`);
   266	    if (calibration.R !== L.PROTOCOL_FE.R) throw new Error("calibration was run with a different R");
   267	    const lock = { kind: "niche-fe-protocol-lock", protocolHash: L.protocolHashFe(digest, L.PROTOCOL_FE), registryDigest: digest, seeds: L.enumerateSeedsFe(L.PROTOCOL_FE), calibrationSha256: await sha256(readFileSync(CALIBRATION_FE)), planSha256: await sha256(readFileSync(PLAN_FE)), sources: await hashSources(), note: "written before the fresh-seed run; fresh results never tune the protocol", frozenAt: new Date().toISOString() };
   268	    writeAtomic(LOCK_FE, serialize(lock));
   269	    console.log(`wrote protocol.lock.json ${lock.protocolHash}`);
   270	    return;
   271	  }
   272	  if (mode === "--write") {
   273	    if (existsSync(EVIDENCE_FE) && !argv.includes("--force")) throw new Error("evidence.json exists; files are written once. Pass --force to rewrite deliberately.");
   274	    const payload = (await generateFe()) as any;
   275	    const seconds = (Date.now() - t0) / 1000;
   276	    const text = serialize(payload);
   277	    const manifestText = serialize(await makeManifestFe(payload, text, seconds));
   278	    writeFileSync(`${EVIDENCE_FE}.tmp`, text);
   279	    writeFileSync(`${MANIFEST_FE}.tmp`, manifestText);
   280	    renameSync(`${EVIDENCE_FE}.tmp`, EVIDENCE_FE);
   281	    renameSync(`${MANIFEST_FE}.tmp`, MANIFEST_FE);
   282	    console.log(`wrote evidence.json (${text.length} bytes) seal ${payload.integritySha256} in ${seconds.toFixed(0)} s; verdict: ${payload.fresh.panel.verdict}`);
   283	    return;
   284	  }
   285	  if (mode === "--check" || mode === "--replay-check") {
   286	    const problems = mode === "--check" ? await checkSavedFe() : await replayCheckFe();
   287	    if (problems.length) {
   288	      console.error(problems.join("\n"));
   289	      process.exit(1);
   290	    }
   291	    console.log(`rung fe ${mode} ok${mode === "--replay-check" ? ` (${Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000)} s)` : ""}`);
   292	    return;
   293	  }
   294	  if (mode === "--repin-rung1") return repinRung1();
   295	  console.error("usage: node tools/niche/niche.ts --rung fe --bench | --calibrate | --freeze | --write | --check | --replay-check | --repin-rung1");
   296	  process.exit(2);
   297	}

    21	export const REPO = fileURLToPath(new URL("../../", import.meta.url));

    29	/** PLAN G6: regeneration (blocks, dev wall controls and fixtures) should take under five minutes on an unloaded machine. Load-dependent, so over-target is reported loudly rather than failing a replay. */

    31	const runtimeNote = (seconds: number): string => (seconds > RUNTIME_TARGET_SECONDS ? ` WARNING: over the ${RUNTIME_TARGET_SECONDS} s target (G6); check machine load before trusting the budget` : ` (within the ${RUNTIME_TARGET_SECONDS} s target)`);

    34	let hooked = false;
    35	export function registerAliases(): void {
    36	  if (hooked || process.env.VITEST) return;
    37	  hooked = true;
    38	  try {
    39	    registerHooks({
    40	      resolve(specifier, context, next) {
    41	        const m = /^@bl\/(.+)$/.exec(specifier);
    42	        return m ? next(pathToFileURL(`${REPO}packages/${m[1]}/src/index.ts`).href, context) : next(specifier, context);
    43	      },
    44	    });
    45	  } catch { /* already running under a loader that resolves @bl/* (vitest) */ }
    46	}

    48	const lib = async () => {
    49	  registerAliases();
    50	  const [evidence, protocol, schema, decision, assay, fixtures] = await Promise.all([
    51	    import("../../packages/runner/src/niche/evidence.ts"),
    52	    import("../../packages/runner/src/niche/protocol.ts"),
    53	    import("../../packages/schema/src/index.ts"),
    54	    import("../../packages/runner/src/niche/decision.ts"),

    60	
    61	export const serialize = (p: unknown): string => `${JSON.stringify(p, null, 1)}\n`;
    62	export const sha256 = async (text: string | Uint8Array): Promise<string> => {
    63	  const { schema } = await lib();
    64	  return schema.sha256HexJS(typeof text === "string" ? new TextEncoder().encode(text) : text);
    65	};
    66	/**
    67	 * Reservations made by slices after this one are not part of this protocol's registry binding: the digest is of the registry as it stood
    68	 * when this slice froze, so a later slice reserving its own seeds does not invalidate this evidence. Collisions with those reservations
    69	 * are still checked against the live registry below. A further slice adds its owner prefix here (or scopes its own digest to its own reservations, as I08 does).
    70	 */
    71	const LATER_SLICE_OWNERS = ["i08 moving light", "i06b free-energy-matched"];
    72	export const registryDigest = async (root = REPO): Promise<string> => {
    73	  const text = readFileSync(`${root}experiments/discovery/seed-registry.json`, "utf8");
    74	  return sha256(text.split("\n").filter((line) => !LATER_SLICE_OWNERS.some((o) => line.includes(`"owner": "${o}`))).join("\n"));
    75	};
    76	
    77	/** Files whose bytes feed the evidence: the library, the simulator and schema it reads, and this tool. */
    78	const SOURCE_DIRS = [SRC_DIR, "packages/schema/src/", "packages/sim-ref/src/"];
    79	export function sourceFiles(root = REPO): string[] {
    80	  const dirs = SOURCE_DIRS.flatMap((d) => readdirSync(`${root}${d}`).filter((f) => f.endsWith(".ts")).sort().map((f) => d + f));
    81	  return [...dirs, "tools/niche/niche.ts"];
    82	}

    84	export async function generate(): Promise<unknown> {
    85	  const L = await lib();
    86	  return L.buildPayload(await registryDigest());
    87	}
    88	
    89	export async function makeManifest(payload: any, evidenceText: string, seconds: number): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
    90	  const sources: Record<string, string> = {};
    91	  for (const f of sourceFiles()) sources[f] = await sha256(readFileSync(REPO + f));
    92	  return {
    93	    kind: "niche-evidence-manifest",
    94	    payloadFileSha256: await sha256(evidenceText),
    95	    integritySha256: payload.integritySha256,
    96	    protocolHash: payload.protocolHash,
    97	    registryDigest: payload.registryDigest,
    98	    stepProjection: payload.stepProjection,
    99	    sources,
   100	    provenance: { note: "not replayed; excluded from the deterministic payload", baseCommit: BASE_COMMIT, node: process.version, platform: `${platform()}-${arch()}`, regenerationSeconds: Math.round(seconds), generatedAt: new Date().toISOString() },
   101	  };
   102	}

   104	/** Validate saved artifacts without simulating. Returns problems (empty when sound). */
   105	export async function checkSaved(paths = { evidence: EVIDENCE, manifest: MANIFEST, calibration: CALIBRATION, lock: LOCK }, root = REPO): Promise<string[]> {
   106	  const L = await lib();
   107	  const problems: string[] = [];
   108	  for (const f of Object.values(paths)) if (!existsSync(f)) problems.push(`${f} is missing`);
   109	  if (problems.length) return problems;
   110	  const text = readFileSync(paths.evidence, "utf8");
   111	  const payload = JSON.parse(text);
   112	  const manifest = JSON.parse(readFileSync(paths.manifest, "utf8"));
   113	  const lock = JSON.parse(readFileSync(paths.lock, "utf8"));
   114	  const calibration = JSON.parse(readFileSync(paths.calibration, "utf8"));
   115	  const canon = L.schema.canonicalJSON;
   116	  if (!L.verifySeal(payload)) problems.push("integritySha256 does not match the payload");
   117	  if (manifest.payloadFileSha256 !== (await sha256(text))) problems.push("manifest payloadFileSha256 does not match evidence.json");
   118	  if (manifest.integritySha256 !== payload.integritySha256) problems.push("manifest seal differs from the payload seal");
   119	  if (manifest.protocolHash !== payload.protocolHash) problems.push("manifest protocolHash differs from the payload");
   120	  // The protocol was frozen (lock written) before the fresh run, and nothing changed since.
   121	  const digest = await registryDigest(root);
   122	  if (payload.registryDigest !== digest) problems.push("seed-registry.json changed since the evidence was written");
   123	  if (lock.protocolHash !== payload.protocolHash) problems.push("protocol.lock.json does not match the payload protocol hash");
   124	  if (lock.protocolHash !== L.protocolHash(digest, L.PROTOCOL)) problems.push("the protocol in code differs from the frozen lock");
   125	  if (canon(payload.protocol) !== canon(L.PROTOCOL)) problems.push("payload protocol differs from the protocol in code");
   126	  if (canon(lock.seeds) !== canon({ dev: L.PROTOCOL.devSeeds, replicates: L.PROTOCOL.devReplicateSeeds, wall: L.PROTOCOL.wallSeeds, fresh: L.PROTOCOL.freshSeeds })) problems.push("lock seed lists differ from the protocol");
   127	  // Dev results are reported separately and match the calibration file the protocol was frozen on.
   128	  if (canon(calibration) !== canon(payload.dev)) problems.push("calibration.json differs from the dev section of evidence.json");
   129	  if (lock.calibrationSha256 !== (await sha256(readFileSync(paths.calibration)))) problems.push("lock calibrationSha256 does not match calibration.json");
   130	  for (const c of payload.dev.candidates) if (L.PROTOCOL.delta[String(c.cluster)] !== c.deltaProposed) problems.push(`delta for cluster ${c.cluster} (${L.PROTOCOL.delta[String(c.cluster)]}) is not the dev replicate floor (${c.deltaProposed})`);
   131	  // Seed hygiene.
   132	  const registry = JSON.parse(readFileSync(`${root}experiments/discovery/seed-registry.json`, "utf8")).reservations as { first: number; last: number; owner: string }[];
   133	  const all = [...L.PROTOCOL.devSeeds, ...L.PROTOCOL.devReplicateSeeds, ...L.PROTOCOL.wallSeeds, ...L.PROTOCOL.freshSeeds, ...L.FIXTURE_SEEDS].map((seed, i) => ({ id: `s${i}`, seed }));
   134	  const own = "i06 niche construction";
   135	  const foreign = L.schema.seedCollisions(all, registry.filter((r) => !r.owner.startsWith(own)), own);
   136	  for (const f of foreign) problems.push(`seed collision: ${f}`);
   137	  // Decision recomputation from the stored inputs.
   138	  const recomputed = payload.fresh.ran === false ? payload.fresh.results : L.decideCandidates(payload.fresh.candidates.map((c: any) => c.input), payload.dev.wall.positiveControlPassed);
   139	  if (canon(recomputed) !== canon(payload.fresh.results)) problems.push("fresh results do not follow the stated decision rules");
   140	  if (canon(L.panelVerdict(recomputed)) !== canon(payload.fresh.panel)) problems.push("panel verdict does not follow the stated rules");
   141	  // Every ledger exactly zero.
   142	  const blocks = [...payload.dev.candidates, ...payload.fresh.candidates].flatMap((c: any) => c.blocks);
   143	  for (const b of blocks) for (const [arm, recs] of Object.entries<any[]>(b.transfers)) for (const r of recs) if (r.residual !== "0" || r.matterResidual !== "0") problems.push(`block ${b.seed} arm ${arm} op ${r.op}: nonzero residual`);
   144	  // Manifest sources.
   145	  const listed = Object.keys(manifest.sources as Record<string, string>);
   146	  if (existsSync(root + SRC_DIR)) for (const f of sourceFiles(root)) if (!listed.includes(f)) problems.push(`source ${f} is not in the manifest (added since the evidence was written)`);
   147	  for (const [file, hash] of Object.entries<string>(manifest.sources)) {
   148	    if (!existsSync(root + file)) problems.push(`source ${file} is missing`);
   149	    else if ((await sha256(readFileSync(root + file))) !== hash) problems.push(`source ${file} changed since the evidence was written`);
   150	  }
   151	  return problems;
   152	}
   153	
   154	export async function replayCheck(evidencePath = EVIDENCE): Promise<string[]> {
   155	  const fresh = serialize(await generate());
   156	  return fresh === readFileSync(evidencePath, "utf8") ? [] : ["regenerated payload differs from evidence.json"];
   157	}

   159	async function bench(): Promise<void> {
   160	  const L = await lib();
   161	  const t0 = Date.now();
   162	  const r = L.runBlock(L.PROTOCOL.panel[0], L.PROTOCOL.devSeeds[0], L.PROTOCOL, L.PROTOCOL.devReplicateSeeds[0]);
   163	  const ms = Date.now() - t0;
   164	  const steps = 2 * L.PROTOCOL.T1 + 12 * L.PROTOCOL.T2; // 2 histories, 10 arms and 2 replicate arms (all steps incl. fixtures are in the projection)
   165	  const proj = L.stepProjection();
   166	  console.log(`block valid=${r.valid}: ${steps} steps in ${ms} ms = ${(ms / steps).toFixed(2)} ms/step`);
   167	  console.log(`projected regeneration: ${proj.steps} steps (${proj.formula}) ~ ${Math.round((proj.steps * ms) / steps / 1000)} s`);
   168	}

   170	async function main(): Promise<void> {
   171	  // Second rung (I06b, docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02): every mode below also exists under --rung fe, in tools/niche/fe.ts.
   172	  const rungAt = process.argv.indexOf("--rung");
   173	  if (rungAt >= 0) {
   174	    if (process.argv[rungAt + 1] !== "fe") throw new Error("--rung takes fe (the second rung); omit it for the first rung");

    61	  });
    62	});
     1	// I06b positive control at realistic scale (PLAN section 4.3): a hand-built polymer wall under the ORDINARY placement rule (no
     2	// overwrite of polymer; same pool-cap and reserve guards), dose ladder ascending, the four gating contrasts per wall seed.
     3	// W1 (ring around the footprint) is the only geometry that placement can legally accommodate; W2 (floor under the receiver) needs
     4	// allowP and is probed here only to record why it is unavailable.
     5	import { receiverAlone, placeAll } from "./arms.ts";
     6	import { medianTimes2, signTestP } from "./decision.ts";
     7	import { buildFreeMatched } from "./freeEnergy.ts";
     8	import { buildWall, controlsAround, nominalBudget, wallCells } from "./fixtures.ts";
     9	import { GATING_FE, PROTOCOL_FE, type GatingFe, type ProtocolFe } from "./protocol.ts";
    10	import { contrastsOfFe, runArmReplicated, type ArmOutcomeFe } from "./replicate.ts";
    11	import { NicheInvalid, cloneAudit, cloneState } from "./transfer.ts";
    12	import type { PreArm } from "./arms.ts";
    13	
    14	export interface WallSeedResult {
    15	  seed: number;
    16	  contrasts: Record<GatingFe, number>;
    17	  /** Y of the wall arm b (summed over replicates) and receiver-present replicates. */
    18	  yWall: number;
    19	  viableReps: number;
    20	}
    21	
    22	/** One wall seed at one dose: wall W1 through every arm and replicate under ordinary placement. */
    23	export function wallGateBlockFe(seed: number, totalP: number, p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): WallSeedResult {
    24	  const pp: ProtocolFe = { ...p, wall: { ...p.wall, totalP } };
    25	  const base = receiverAlone({ audit: nominalBudget(seed, pp) }, seed, pp);
    26	  const wall: PreArm = { id: "b", state: cloneState(base.state), audit: cloneAudit(base.audit), records: [] };
    27	  buildWall(wall, wallCells("W1", pp), totalP, pp);
    28	  const arms = [...controlsAround(wall, base, seed, pp)];
    29	  const fm = buildFreeMatched(arms[0], pp);
    30	  if (fm.arms.length === 0) throw new NicheInvalid("every free-energy-matched comparator was refused");
    31	  arms.push(...fm.arms);
    32	  placeAll(arms, pp, { allowP: false });
    33	  const out: Record<string, ArmOutcomeFe> = {};
    34	  for (const x of arms) out[x.id] = runArmReplicated(x, seed, pp, pp.R);
    35	  const c = contrastsOfFe(out);
    36	  return { seed, contrasts: { builder: c.builder, poly: c.poly, space: c.space, inoc2: c.inoc2 }, yWall: out.b.y, viableReps: out.b.survivedReps };
    37	}
    38	
    39	export interface DoseRow {
    40	  totalP: number;
    41	  seeds: readonly number[];
    42	  results: WallSeedResult[];
    43	  /** Per gating contrast: seeds positive and twice the median difference. */
    44	  stats: Record<GatingFe, { k: number; p: { num: number; den: number }; medianTimes2: number }>;
    45	  /** The gating contrast with the smallest median effect: where G-NOISE is read. */
    46	  limiting: GatingFe;
    47	  limitingMedianTimes2: number;
    48	}
    49	
    50	export function doseRow(totalP: number, results: WallSeedResult[]): DoseRow {
    51	  const stats = Object.fromEntries(
    52	    GATING_FE.map((k) => {
    53	      const d = results.map((r) => r.contrasts[k]);
    54	      const kk = d.filter((x) => x > 0).length;
    55	      return [k, { k: kk, p: signTestP(d.length, kk), medianTimes2: medianTimes2(d) }];
    56	    }),
    57	  ) as DoseRow["stats"];
    58	  const limiting = GATING_FE.reduce((m, k) => (stats[k].medianTimes2 < stats[m].medianTimes2 ? k : m), GATING_FE[0]);
    59	  return { totalP, seeds: results.map((r) => r.seed), results, stats, limiting, limitingMedianTimes2: stats[limiting].medianTimes2 };
    60	}
    61	
    62	/** Wall pass at a dose for a candidate: every gating contrast positive in at least wallPassMin seeds and median >= delta. */
    63	export function wallPasses(row: DoseRow, delta: number, p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): boolean {
    64	  return GATING_FE.every((k) => row.stats[k].k >= p.wallPassMin && row.stats[k].medianTimes2 >= 2 * delta);
    65	}
    66	/** G-NOISE: delta at most 1/noiseDivisor of the limiting contrast's median effect (twice-median integers: medianTimes2 >= 2 * noiseDivisor * delta). */
    67	export function noiseGate(row: DoseRow, delta: number, p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): boolean {
    68	  return row.limitingMedianTimes2 >= 2 * p.noiseDivisor * delta;
    69	}
    70	
    71	export interface Qualification {
    72	  cluster: number;
    73	  delta: number;
    74	  /** 2 x the median dev builder polymer at T1 = twice-the-median integer builderPMedianTimes2. */
    75	  ceiling: number;
    76	  /** Smallest dose at which the wall passes ignoring the ceiling (the minimum detectable dose), or null if none on the ladder. */
    77	  minDetectableDose: number | null;
    78	  /** Smallest dose <= ceiling where the wall passes and G-NOISE holds, or null. */
    79	  qualifyingDose: number | null;
    80	  /** Doses <= ceiling on the ladder. */
    81	  dosesWithinCeiling: number[];
    82	  qualified: boolean;
    83	  reason: string;
    84	}
    85	
    86	export function qualify(cluster: number, delta: number, builderPMedianTimes2: number, rows: readonly DoseRow[], p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): Qualification {
    87	  const ceiling = builderPMedianTimes2;
    88	  const within = rows.filter((r) => r.totalP <= ceiling);
    89	  const mdd = rows.find((r) => wallPasses(r, delta, p)) ?? null;
    90	  const q = within.find((r) => wallPasses(r, delta, p) && noiseGate(r, delta, p)) ?? null;
    91	  const reason = q
    92	    ? `W1 passes at ${q.totalP} (<= ceiling ${ceiling}) and delta ${delta} is at most half of the limiting contrast (${q.limiting}) median effect`
    93	    : mdd === null
    94	      ? `W1 is not detected at any ladder dose up to ${rows.length ? rows[rows.length - 1].totalP : 0} (passing needs ${p.wallPassMin} of ${p.wallSeeds.length} seeds positive on all four contrasts and median >= delta ${delta})`
    95	      : mdd.totalP > ceiling
    96	        ? `minimum detectable dose ${mdd.totalP} exceeds the realistic ceiling ${ceiling} (2 x dev builder polymer median)`
    97	        : `W1 passes at ${mdd.totalP} but delta ${delta} exceeds half of the limiting contrast (${mdd.limiting}) median effect ${mdd.limitingMedianTimes2 / 2} (G-NOISE)`;
    98	  return { cluster, delta, ceiling, minDetectableDose: mdd?.totalP ?? null, qualifyingDose: q?.totalP ?? null, dosesWithinCeiling: within.map((r) => r.totalP), qualified: q !== null, reason };
    99	}
   100	
   101	export interface LadderReport {
   102	  geometry: "W1";
   103	  rows: DoseRow[];
   104	  w2: { status: "unavailable"; reason: string } | { status: "available"; reason: string };
   105	}
   106	
   107	/** Probe W2 (polymer floor under the receiver) under ordinary placement: expected to refuse, which is recorded as the reason it is unavailable. */
   108	export function probeW2(p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): LadderReport["w2"] {
   109	  const seed = p.wallSeeds[0];
   110	  const pp: ProtocolFe = { ...p, wall: { ...p.wall, totalP: p.doses[0] } };
   111	  const base = receiverAlone({ audit: nominalBudget(seed, pp) }, seed, pp);
   112	  const wall: PreArm = { id: "b", state: cloneState(base.state), audit: cloneAudit(base.audit), records: [] };
   113	  buildWall(wall, wallCells("W2", pp), p.doses[0], pp);
   114	  try {
   115	    placeAll([wall, base], pp, { allowP: false });
   116	    return { status: "available", reason: "ordinary placement found enough common cells over the floor" };
   117	  } catch (e) {
   118	    if (e instanceof NicheInvalid) return { status: "unavailable", reason: `ordinary placement refuses a polymer floor under the receiver: ${e.reason}` };
   119	    throw e;
   120	  }
   121	}
   122	
   123	/**
   124	 * A candidate is settled once the ladder cannot teach more about it: it has qualified (a dose within its ceiling passes the wall
   125	 * rule and G-NOISE), or it has a passing dose (its minimum detectable dose is known) and every dose within its ceiling has run.
   126	 * Otherwise the ladder continues, to the top if need be, to state the minimum detectable dose or its absence.
   127	 */
   128	export function settled(rows: readonly DoseRow[], delta: number, ceiling: number, p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): boolean {
   129	  if (rows.some((r) => r.totalP <= ceiling && wallPasses(r, delta, p) && noiseGate(r, delta, p))) return true;
   130	  const eligibleDone = p.doses.filter((d) => d <= ceiling).every((d) => rows.some((r) => r.totalP === d));
   131	  return eligibleDone && rows.some((r) => wallPasses(r, delta, p));
   132	}
   133	
   134	/** Run the ladder ascending until every candidate is settled (see settled) or the ladder is exhausted. */
   135	export function runLadder(deltas: Readonly<Record<string, number>>, ceilings: Readonly<Record<string, number>>, p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE, onDose?: (row: DoseRow) => void): LadderReport {
   136	  const rows: DoseRow[] = [];
   137	  for (const dose of p.doses) {
   138	    const row = doseRow(dose, p.wallSeeds.map((s) => wallGateBlockFe(s, dose, p)));
   139	    rows.push(row);
   140	    onDose?.(row);
   141	    if (Object.keys(deltas).every((c) => settled(rows, deltas[c], ceilings[c], p))) break;
   142	  }
   143	  return { geometry: "W1", rows, w2: probeW2(p) };
   144	}

     1	// Stage 2 and the readouts. Observers are read-only: they look at sim.state and sim.roles after each step and never write.
     2	import { CH, G, cellCount, packLineageLo, stateHash, totalsOf, validateState, type WorldState } from "@bl/schema";

     8	export interface ArmOutcome {
     9	  id: ArmId;
    10	  /** Receiver-labelled biomass inventory B at the start of Stage 2 and at each census (last entry is T2). */
    11	  receiverB: number[];
    12	  /** Receiver-labelled B at T2: the primary endpoint Y. */
    13	  y: number;
    14	  survived: boolean;
    15	  builderB: number;
    16	  /** Receiver-cell PHOTO + GROW quanta summed over every Stage 2 step (synthesis), and DECOMP, RESP. null = unavailable: the co-culture arm (a) has no accurate producing-cell ownership (reactions are counted after the step, and injury can clear a lineage after it reacted), so it is never attributed. */
    17	  synthesis: number | null;
    18	  decomp: number | null;
    19	  resp: number | null;
    20	  /** A role counter reached its saturation value at some step: flux attribution is unavailable. */
    21	  saturated: boolean;
    22	  /** Y - B_r gain is at least half synthesis: "growth" may be said. Otherwise "inventory benefit, growth not attributed". */
    23	  growthAttributed: boolean;
    24	  energyEnd: string;
    25	  endHash: string;
    26	}
    27	
    28	export interface Hooks {
    29	  /** Called after each step with the state and the roles buffer (read only). */
    30	  onStep?: (state: WorldState, roles: Uint32Array, step: number) => void;
    31	}
    32	
    33	const lin = (s: WorldState, raw: number) => packLineageLo(s.cfg, raw);
    34	
    35	function receiverB(s: WorldState, raw: number): number {
    36	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
    37	  const lo = lin(s, raw);
    38	  let t = 0;
    39	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) if (s.genome[G.LIN_HI * n + i] === 0 && s.genome[G.LIN_LO * n + i] === lo) t += s.cells[CH.B * n + i];
    40	  return t;
    41	}
    42	
    43	/** Run Stage 2 on a placed arm for T2 steps; throws NicheInvalid on any residual. */
    44	export function runArm(x: PreArm, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL, hooks: Hooks = {}): ArmOutcome {
    45	  const sim = new RefSim(x.state);
    46	  const n = cellCount(x.state.cfg);
    47	  const blo = lin(x.state, p.builderRaw);
    48	  const y0 = receiverB(sim.state, p.receiverRaw);
    49	  const series = [y0];
    50	  let synthesis = 0, decomp = 0, resp = 0, saturated = false;
    51	  for (let t = 1; t <= p.T2; t++) {
    52	    sim.step();
    53	    const s = sim.state, roles = sim.roles;
    54	    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    55	      const w0 = roles[2 * i], w1 = roles[2 * i + 1];
    56	      if ((w0 & 0xffff) >= p.roleSaturation || w0 >>> 16 >= p.roleSaturation || (w1 & 0xffff) >= p.roleSaturation || w1 >>> 16 >= p.roleSaturation) saturated = true;
    57	      // Roles are counted on every cell where the builder was removed (only receivers react there; injury may clear a lineage after it synthesized). The co-culture arm would need pre-reaction producing-cell ownership, which is not observed, so it is reported unavailable.
    58	      if (x.id !== "a") {
    59	        synthesis += (w0 & 0xffff) + (w0 >>> 16);
    60	        decomp += w1 & 0xffff;
    61	        resp += w1 >>> 16;
    62	      }
    63	    }
    64	    hooks.onStep?.(s, roles, s.step);
    65	    if (t % p.census === 0 || t === p.T2) {
    66	      assertAudit(s, x.audit, `stage 2 ${x.id} step ${t}`);
    67	      series.push(receiverB(s, p.receiverRaw));
    68	    }
    69	  }
    70	  const s = sim.state;
    71	  const errs = validateState(s);
    72	  if (errs.length) throw new NicheInvalid(`stage 2 ${x.id}: ${errs[0]}`);
    73	  let builderB = 0;
    74	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) if (s.genome[G.LIN_HI * n + i] === 0 && s.genome[G.LIN_LO * n + i] === blo) builderB += s.cells[CH.B * n + i];
    75	  const y = series[series.length - 1];
    76	  const gain = y - y0;
    77	  return {
    78	    id: x.id,
    79	    receiverB: series,
    80	    y,
    81	    survived: y > 0,
    82	    builderB,
    83	    synthesis: x.id === "a" ? null : synthesis,
    84	    decomp: x.id === "a" ? null : decomp,
    85	    resp: x.id === "a" ? null : resp,
    86	    saturated,
    87	    growthAttributed: x.id !== "a" && !saturated && gain > 0 && 2 * synthesis >= gain,
    88	    energyEnd: `${totalsOf(s.cfg, s.cells).energy}`,
    89	    endHash: stateHash(s),
    90	  };
    91	}

    93	export interface HistoryCost {
    94	  kind: "builder" | "nonbuilder";
    95	  B: string;
    96	  P: string;
    97	  E: string;
    98	  A: string;
    99	  C: string;
   100	  lightIn: string;
   101	  heatOut: string;
   102	  builderLineageB: number;
   103	  living: number;
   104	  /** kGene maintenance is not separable from catalytic and ordinary maintenance in react(); stated, not inferred. */
   105	  kGeneMaintenance: "unavailable";
   106	}
   107	
   108	export function historyCost(kind: "builder" | "nonbuilder", s: WorldState, p: Protocol): HistoryCost {
   109	  const t = totalsOf(s.cfg, s.cells);
   110	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
   111	  let b = 0, living = 0;
   112	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
   113	    const hi = s.genome[G.LIN_HI * n + i], lo = s.genome[G.LIN_LO * n + i];
   114	    if ((hi | lo) !== 0) living++;
   115	    if (hi === 0 && lo === lin(s, p.builderRaw)) b += s.cells[CH.B * n + i];
   116	  }
   117	  return { kind, B: `${t.B}`, P: `${t.P}`, E: `${t.E}`, A: `${t.A}`, C: `${t.C}`, lightIn: `${s.lightIn}`, heatOut: `${s.heatOut}`, builderLineageB: b, living, kGeneMaintenance: "unavailable" };
   118	}

   150	  const y = (id: ArmId): number => {

    15	export function fixtureProtocolFe(p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): ProtocolFe {

    19	const setChannel = (s: WorldState, ch: number, cells: readonly number[], total: number): void => {
    20	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg), m = cells.length, base = Math.floor(total / m), rem = total % m;
    21	  cells.forEach((i, j) => (s.cells[ch * n + i] += base + (j < rem ? 1 : 0)));
    22	};

    24	export interface FeBlock {
    25	  outcomes: Record<string, ArmOutcomeFe>;
    26	  contrasts: ContrastsFe;
    27	  freeBefore: FreeRow[];
    28	  freeAfter: FreeRow[];
    29	  refused: { id: string; reason: string }[];
    30	}
    31	
    32	/** Controls around a hand-built conditioned arm `b`, plus fU/fS/fB; shared placement; R replicates of every arm. */
    33	export function runFeArms(b: PreArm, d0: PreArm, seed: number, p: ProtocolFe, opts: { allowP?: boolean; genome?: Genome } = {}): FeBlock {
    34	  const arms = [...controlsAround(b, d0, seed, p)];
    35	  const fm = buildFreeMatched(arms[0], p);
    36	  if (fm.arms.length === 0) throw new NicheInvalid("every free-energy-matched comparator was refused");
    37	  arms.push(...fm.arms);
    38	  const freeBefore = freeRows(arms);
    39	  placeAll(arms, p, opts);
    40	  const freeAfter = freeRows(arms);
    41	  const outcomes: Record<string, ArmOutcomeFe> = {};
    42	  for (const x of arms) outcomes[x.id] = runArmReplicated(x, seed, p, p.R);
    43	  return { outcomes, contrasts: contrastsOfFe(outcomes), freeBefore, freeAfter, refused: fm.refused };
    44	}
    45	
    46	const condOf = (base: PreArm): PreArm => ({ id: "b", state: cloneState(base.state), audit: cloneAudit(base.audit), records: [] });
    47	
    48	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    49	// energy fields
    50	
    51	export type FieldVariant = "far-field" | "uniform" | "footprint";
    52	/** Extra stored free energy, sized so the naive metric clears the fixture delta. */
    53	export const FIELD_GIFT: Record<FieldVariant, number> = { "far-field": 10000, uniform: 10000, footprint: 4000 };
    54	
    55	/** Cells of the far-field band: outside the footprint-plus-halo region, within two more cells of radius. */
    56	export function bandCells(p: ProtocolFe): number[] {
    57	  const inR = new Set(regionCells(p));
    58	  return disc(p.footprint.x, p.footprint.y, p.footprint.radius + p.halo + 2, p.grid, p.grid).filter((i) => !inR.has(i));
    59	}
    60	
    61	export function fieldBlock(variant: FieldVariant, seed: number, p: ProtocolFe, gift = FIELD_GIFT[variant]): FeBlock {
    62	  const base = receiverAlone({ audit: uniformBudget(p) }, seed, p);
    63	  const cond = condOf(base);
    64	  const cells = variant === "far-field" ? bandCells(p) : variant === "uniform" ? Array.from({ length: p.grid * p.grid }, (_, i) => i) : footprintCells(p);
    65	  setChannel(cond.state, CH.E, cells, gift);
    66	  cond.audit.energyStart += BigInt(gift);
    67	  return runFeArms(cond, base, seed, p);
    68	}
    69	
    70	/** Non-living detritus B on the halo ring (funded from A there and from E outside): bound energy instead of free, equal totals with receiver alone. An inert receiver is placed. */
    71	export function detritusField(seed: number, p: ProtocolFe, detritus = 200): { base: PreArm; cond: PreArm } {
    72	  const base = receiverAlone({ audit: uniformBudget(p) }, seed, p);
    73	  const cond = condOf(base);
    74	  const ring = regionCellsExcludingFootprint(p);
    75	  audited("detritus", cond.state, cond.audit, () => {
    76	    const s = cond.state;
    77	    debitEven(s, CH.A, detritus, ring);
    78	    setChannel(s, CH.B, ring, detritus);
    79	    debitEven(s, CH.E, (s.cfg.eB - s.cfg.eA) * detritus, outsideRegion(p));
    80	    return { detritus };
    81	  });
    82	  return { base, cond };
    83	}
    84	export const takeoverBlockFe = (seed: number, p: ProtocolFe): FeBlock => {
    85	  const { base, cond } = detritusField(seed, p);
    86	  return runFeArms(cond, base, seed, p, { genome: inertReceiver(p) });
    87	};
    88	export const nullBlockFe = (seed: number, p: ProtocolFe): FeBlock => {
    89	  const base = receiverAlone({ audit: uniformBudget(p) }, seed, p);
    90	  return runFeArms(condOf(base), base, seed, p);
    91	};
    92	
    93	/** The rung-1 hand wall W2 (polymer floor under the receiver, allowP) at the frozen scale through the second-rung arms: a diagnostic, never qualifying. */
    94	export function wallGateBlockFe2(seed: number, p: ProtocolFe): FeBlock {
    95	  const base = receiverAlone({ audit: nominalBudget(seed, p) }, seed, p);
    96	  const wall = condOf(base);
    97	  buildWall(wall, wallCells("W2", p), p.wall.totalP, p);
    98	  return runFeArms(wall, base, seed, p, { allowP: true });
    99	}
   100	
   101	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   102	// free-gap-replay
   103	
   104	export interface FreeGapSeed {
   105	  seed: number;
   106	  /** Rung-1 observed direction: receiver alone holds MORE free E than b with equal totals. */
   107	  d0FreeE: string;
   108	  bFreeE: string;
   109	  d0Total: string;
   110	  bTotal: string;
   111	  d0HoldsMoreFree: boolean;
   112	  totalsEqual: boolean;
   113	  /** Free-energy difference to b for each comparator, before and after placement (all zero). */
   114	  freeDiffBefore: Record<string, string>;
   115	  freeDiffAfter: Record<string, string>;
   116	  constructionGap: Record<string, string>;
   117	  flags: Record<string, string>;
   118	  residualsZero: boolean;
   119	}
   120	
   121	export function freeGapSeed(seed: number, p: ProtocolFe): FreeGapSeed {
   122	  const { base, cond } = detritusField(seed, p);
   123	  const b = runFeArmsPre(cond, base, seed, p);
   124	  const row = (rows: FreeRow[], id: string) => rows.find((r) => r.id === id)!;
   125	  const ids = ["fU", "fS", "fB"].filter((id) => b.before.some((r) => r.id === id));
   126	  const d0 = row(b.before, "d0"), bb = row(b.before, "b");
   127	  return {
   128	    seed,
   129	    d0FreeE: d0.freeE,
   130	    bFreeE: bb.freeE,
   131	    d0Total: d0.total,
   132	    bTotal: bb.total,
   133	    d0HoldsMoreFree: BigInt(d0.freeE) > BigInt(bb.freeE),
   134	    totalsEqual: d0.total === bb.total,
   135	    freeDiffBefore: Object.fromEntries(ids.map((id) => [id, row(b.before, id).freeGapToB])),
   136	    freeDiffAfter: Object.fromEntries(ids.map((id) => [id, row(b.after, id).freeGapToB])),
   137	    constructionGap: Object.fromEntries(["d0", ...ids].map((id) => [id, row(b.before, id).constructionGap])),
   138	    flags: Object.fromEntries(["d0", ...ids].map((id) => [id, row(b.before, id).flag])),
   139	    residualsZero: b.residualsZero,
   140	  };
   141	}
   142	
   143	/** Arms and free-energy rows only, no Stage 2 (the replay is about construction). */
   144	function runFeArmsPre(b: PreArm, d0: PreArm, seed: number, p: ProtocolFe): { before: FreeRow[]; after: FreeRow[]; residualsZero: boolean } {
   145	  const arms = [...controlsAround(b, d0, seed, p)];
   146	  const fm = buildFreeMatched(arms[0], p);
   147	  arms.push(...fm.arms);
   148	  const before = freeRows(arms);
   149	  placeAll(arms, p, { genome: inertReceiver(p) });
   150	  const after = freeRows(arms);
   151	  const residualsZero = arms.every((x) => x.records.every((r) => r.residual === "0" && r.matterResidual === "0"));
   152	  return { before, after, residualsZero };
   153	}
   154	
   155	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   156	// guards
   157	
   158	export interface GuardFe {
   159	  id: string;
   160	  ok: boolean;
   161	  detail: string;
   162	}
   163	export function runGuardsFe(p: ProtocolFe): GuardFe[] {
   164	  const out: GuardFe[] = [];
   165	  const attempt = (id: string, fn: () => void, want: RegExp): void => {
   166	    try {
   167	      fn();
   168	      out.push({ id, ok: false, detail: "no refusal" });
   169	    } catch (e) {
   170	      out.push({ id, ok: e instanceof NicheInvalid && want.test(e.reason), detail: e instanceof NicheInvalid ? e.reason : String(e) });
   171	    }
   172	  };
   173	  attempt("credit-overdraw", () => {
   174	    const base = receiverAlone({ audit: uniformBudget(p) }, 1, p);
   175	    const f = buildFreeMatched(condOf(base), p).arms.find((x) => x.id === "fB")!;
   176	    fundBiomass(f, 10 ** 9, outsideRegion(p));
   177	  }, /credit-overdraw/);
   178	  return out;
   179	}
   180	
   181	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   182	// fixture decisions
   183	
   184	export interface FixtureReportFe {
   185	  id: string;
   186	  seeds: readonly number[];
   187	  contrasts: ContrastsFe[];
   188	  naiveK: number;
   189	  naiveWouldSay: "benefit" | "no benefit";
   190	  /** The first rung's inoculum contrast alone passes its sign test with median >= delta: it is fooled. */
   191	  inocFooledOld: boolean;
   192	  /** The free-energy-matched inoculum contrast does not pass. */
   193	  inocCaughtNew: boolean;
   194	  kOld: number;
   195	  kNew: number;
   196	  gatingK: Record<GatingFe, number>;
   197	  /** Gating contrasts that fail (these reject the field in the full gate). */
   198	  caughtBy: GatingFe[];
   199	  fullGateLabel: CandidateResultFe["label"];
   200	  synthesis: number[];
   201	  /** Free energy of every arm for the first seed, before placement (the 9,917 vs 22,169 style table). */
   202	  freeBefore: FreeRow[];
   203	  freeAfter: FreeRow[];
   204	}
   205	
   206	export function decideFixtureFe(id: string, seeds: readonly number[], blocks: FeBlock[], delta: number): FixtureReportFe {
   207	  const cs = blocks.map((b) => b.contrasts);
   208	  const col = (k: keyof ContrastsFe) => cs.map((c) => c[k]);
   209	  const n = blocks.length;
   210	  const dec = decideCandidatesFe([{ id, status: "ok", invalidReasons: [], contrasts: { builder: col("builder"), poly: col("poly"), space: col("space"), inoc2: col("inoc2"), spaceP: col("spaceP"), spaceNP: col("spaceNP"), inoc: col("inoc"), inocMatched: col("inocMatched"), naive: col("naive") }, delta, viableBlocks: n, blocks: n, expectedBlocks: n, notSimulatedReasons: [] }], 1)[0];
   211	  const old = contrastResult(col("inoc"), delta, ALPHA), nw = contrastResult(col("inoc2"), delta, ALPHA);
   212	  const naiveK = col("naive").filter((x) => x > 0).length;
   213	  const naivePositive = naiveK * 2 > n && medianTimes2(col("naive")) >= 2 * delta;
   214	  return {
   215	    id,
   216	    seeds,
   217	    contrasts: cs,
   218	    naiveK,
   219	    naiveWouldSay: naivePositive ? "benefit" : "no benefit",
   220	    inocFooledOld: old.passes,
   221	    inocCaughtNew: !nw.passes,
   222	    kOld: old.k,
   223	    kNew: nw.k,
   224	    gatingK: Object.fromEntries(GATING_FE.map((k) => [k, dec.gating[k].k])) as Record<GatingFe, number>,
   225	    caughtBy: GATING_FE.filter((k) => !dec.gating[k].passes),
   226	    fullGateLabel: dec.label,
   227	    synthesis: blocks.map((b) => b.outcomes.b?.synthesis ?? 0),
   228	    freeBefore: blocks[0].freeBefore,
   229	    freeAfter: blocks[0].freeAfter,
   230	  };
   231	}

   103	/** The four gating contrasts of the second rung: inoc2 replaces the first rung's inoc. */
   104	export const GATING_FE = ["builder", "poly", "space", "inoc2"] as const;
   105	export type GatingFe = (typeof GATING_FE)[number];

   107	export const PROTOCOL_FE = {
   108	  ...PROTOCOL,
   109	  id: "niche-fe-v1",
   110	  question: "builder->receiver dependency edge, second rung: free-energy-matched comparators, replicated readout, ordinary-placement positive control",
   111	  /** Stage-2 replicate sims per arm (block seed + k * stride, k = 0..R-1); Y is the integer sum over them. At most 4: the replicate-noise control needs a disjoint second set inside the reserved offsets 0..7. */
   112	  R: 4,
   113	  replicateStride: 1000,
   114	  /** Largest offset index reserved in the registry (R = 8 would use all of them for the primary readout). */
   115	  maxOffsetIndex: 7,
   116	  /** Replicates used by the fixtures (unit scale, cheap). */
   117	  fixtureR: 2,
   118	  /** Field arm viable (receiver B above zero) in at least R - 1 of R replicates counts the block; at least this many of the fresh blocks. */
   119	  viableBlocksMin: 14,
   120	  seedRange: { first: 8_601_001, last: 8_608_999 },
   121	  devSeeds: [8_601_001, 8_601_002, 8_601_003, 8_601_004, 8_601_005, 8_601_006, 8_601_007, 8_601_008] as readonly number[],
   122	  devReplicateSeeds: [8_601_201, 8_601_202, 8_601_203, 8_601_204] as readonly number[],
   123	  wallSeeds: [8_601_301, 8_601_302, 8_601_303, 8_601_304, 8_601_305, 8_601_306, 8_601_307, 8_601_308] as readonly number[],
   124	  fixtureSeeds: [8_601_401, 8_601_402, 8_601_403, 8_601_404, 8_601_405, 8_601_406, 8_601_407, 8_601_408] as readonly number[],
   125	  freshSeeds: [8_601_501, 8_601_502, 8_601_503, 8_601_504, 8_601_505, 8_601_506, 8_601_507, 8_601_508, 8_601_509, 8_601_510, 8_601_511, 8_601_512, 8_601_513, 8_601_514, 8_601_515, 8_601_516] as readonly number[],
   126	  /** Per-candidate delta from the dev replicate-noise control (summed-R units); filled in by --calibrate, then frozen here. */
   127	  delta: { "0": 1152, "4": 1031, "9": 1169 } as Readonly<Record<string, number>>,
   128	  /** Positive-control dose ladder: total hand-built polymer, ascending. Qualifying doses are at most the candidate's ceiling (2 x the dev median builder polymer at T1). */
   129	  doses: [60, 120, 240, 600, 1200] as readonly number[],
   130	  /** Wall pass rule on dev: at least this many of the wall seeds positive on each of the four gating contrasts, and the median at least delta_c. */
   131	  wallPassMin: 7,
   132	  /** G-NOISE: delta_c at most 1/noiseDivisor of the limiting contrast's median effect at the qualifying dose. */
   133	  noiseDivisor: 2,
   134	  /** Frozen wording of the power statement (verified exactly in tests; never computed with floats in the payload). */
   135	  powerStatement:
   136	    "With three candidates running, the first Holm rejection needs at least 13 of 16 blocks positive (p = 697/65,536; 12 if only one candidate runs). A contrast whose true per-block positive probability is 0.75 passes that single threshold about 40.5% of the time, 0.85 about 79%, 0.90 about 93%, 0.95 about 99%; four contrasts, delta and viability can only lower the pass rate. A negative is therefore a failure to detect a consistent benefit under these rules (a sign test on D > 0, the median at least delta, four contrasts and viability), not an exclusion bound on the effect size and not evidence that there is no benefit.",
   137	};
   138	export type ProtocolFe = typeof PROTOCOL_FE;
   139	
   140	/** Every seed that can run in this rung: block, replicate (primary and noise-control offsets), wall, fixture. Sorted, unique. */
   141	export function enumerateSeedsFe(p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): number[] {
   142	  const out = new Set<number>();
   143	  const offs = (n: number, s: number) => {
   144	    for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) out.add(s + k * p.replicateStride);
   145	  };
   146	  for (const s of [...p.devSeeds, ...p.freshSeeds, ...p.wallSeeds]) offs(p.R, s);
   147	  for (const s of p.fixtureSeeds) offs(p.fixtureR, s);
   148	  for (const s of p.devReplicateSeeds) offs(2 * p.R, s);
   149	  return [...out].sort((a, b) => a - b);
   150	}
   151	
   152	/** SHA-256 of the canonical second-rung protocol, the registry digest it was checked against, and the enumerated seed list. */
   153	export function protocolHashFe(registryDigest: string, p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): string {
   154	  return sha256HexJS(new TextEncoder().encode(canonicalJSON({ protocol: p, registryDigest, seeds: enumerateSeedsFe(p) })));
   155	}
     1	// I06b edge decision (PLAN section 5.3): the first rung's exact integer sign tests, with 16 blocks, inoc2 in place of inoc, a viability
     2	// requirement on the field arm (no survival-benefit claim), and adequacy per candidate from the ordinary-placement positive control.
     3	// The first rung's decision.ts is not touched.
     4	import type { EvidenceStatus } from "@bl/schema";
     5	import { ALPHA, contrastResult, ratLe, ratMax, signTestP, type ContrastResult, type Rat } from "./decision.ts";
     6	import { GATING_FE, type GatingFe } from "./protocol.ts";
     7	
     8	export interface CandidateInputFe {
     9	  id: string;
    10	  /** "not-simulated": the positive control did not qualify this candidate, so no fresh block was run. */
    11	  status: "ok" | "invalid" | "not-simulated";
    12	  invalidReasons: string[];
    13	  contrasts: Record<GatingFe, number[]> & { spaceP: number[]; spaceNP: number[]; inoc: number[]; inocMatched: number[]; naive: number[] };
    14	  delta: number;
    15	  /** Blocks in which the field arm was viable (receiver present in at least R - 1 of R replicates). */
    16	  viableBlocks: number;
    17	  blocks: number;
    18	  expectedBlocks: number;
    19	  /** Reasons when not simulated: minimum detectable dose, ceiling, floor. */
    20	  notSimulatedReasons: string[];
    21	}
    22	
    23	export interface CandidateResultFe {
    24	  id: string;
    25	  label: EvidenceStatus;
    26	  simulated: boolean;
    27	  mechanism: "polymer-arrangement-attributed" | "non-polymer field pattern" | "combined conditioned field" | "n/a";
    28	  reasons: string[];
    29	  gating: Record<GatingFe, ContrastResult>;
    30	  attribution: { spaceP: ContrastResult; spaceNP: ContrastResult };
    31	  /** Reported only: the first rung's inoculum contrast and the naive contrast. */
    32	  reported: { inoc: ContrastResult; inocMatched: ContrastResult; naive: ContrastResult };
    33	  pCand: Rat;
    34	  holmThreshold: Rat | null;
    35	  holmRejected: boolean;
    36	  viabilityPass: boolean;
    37	}
    38	
    39	export function decideCandidatesFe(cands: readonly CandidateInputFe[], minViable = 14): CandidateResultFe[] {
    40	  const ran = cands.filter((c) => c.status !== "not-simulated");
    41	  const m = ran.length;
    42	  const alphaEach: Rat = { num: ALPHA.num, den: ALPHA.den * Math.max(1, m) };
    43	  const results = new Map<string, CandidateResultFe>();
    44	  const raw = (d: readonly number[], delta: number): ContrastResult => contrastResult(d, delta, { num: 0, den: 1 });
    45	  for (const c of cands) {
    46	    const g = Object.fromEntries(GATING_FE.map((k) => [k, raw(c.contrasts[k], c.delta)])) as Record<GatingFe, ContrastResult>;
    47	    results.set(c.id, {
    48	      id: c.id,
    49	      label: "unsupported-measurement",
    50	      simulated: c.status !== "not-simulated",
    51	      mechanism: "n/a",
    52	      reasons: [],
    53	      gating: g,
    54	      attribution: { spaceP: contrastResult(c.contrasts.spaceP, c.delta, alphaEach), spaceNP: contrastResult(c.contrasts.spaceNP, c.delta, alphaEach) },
    55	      reported: { inoc: raw(c.contrasts.inoc, c.delta), inocMatched: raw(c.contrasts.inocMatched, c.delta), naive: raw(c.contrasts.naive, c.delta) },
    56	      pCand: c.status === "ok" && c.blocks === c.expectedBlocks ? ratMax(GATING_FE.map((k) => g[k].p)) : { num: 1, den: 1 },
    57	      holmThreshold: null,
    58	      holmRejected: false,
    59	      viabilityPass: c.viableBlocks >= minViable,
    60	    });
    61	  }
    62	  const order = ran.map((c) => results.get(c.id)!).sort((a, b) => a.pCand.num * b.pCand.den - b.pCand.num * a.pCand.den || (a.id < b.id ? -1 : 1));
    63	  let stopped = false;
    64	  order.forEach((r, i) => {
    65	    r.holmThreshold = { num: ALPHA.num, den: ALPHA.den * (m - i) };
    66	    if (!stopped && ratLe(r.pCand, r.holmThreshold)) r.holmRejected = true;
    67	    else stopped = true;
    68	  });
    69	  for (const c of ran) {
    70	    const r = results.get(c.id)!;
    71	    for (const k of GATING_FE) r.gating[k].passes = ratLe(r.gating[k].p, r.holmThreshold!) && r.gating[k].medianTimes2 >= 2 * c.delta;
    72	  }
    73	  for (const c of cands) {
    74	    const r = results.get(c.id)!;
    75	    if (c.status === "not-simulated") {
    76	      r.label = "unsupported-measurement";
    77	      r.reasons = c.notSimulatedReasons.length ? c.notSimulatedReasons : ["the ordinary-placement positive control did not qualify this candidate; no fresh block was simulated"];
    78	    } else if (c.status === "invalid" || c.blocks !== c.expectedBlocks) {
    79	      r.label = "invalid-or-incomplete";
    80	      r.reasons = c.invalidReasons.length ? c.invalidReasons : [`${c.blocks} of ${c.expectedBlocks} blocks valid`];
    81	    } else {
    82	      const failed = GATING_FE.filter((k) => !r.gating[k].passes);
    83	      const ok = r.holmRejected && failed.length === 0 && r.viabilityPass;
    84	      r.label = ok ? "supported" : "unsupported-within-tested-domain";
    85	      if (!ok) {
    86	        if (failed.length) r.reasons.push(`contrast(s) not passing: ${failed.join(", ")}`);
    87	        if (!r.holmRejected) r.reasons.push("Holm does not reject the candidate");
    88	        if (!r.viabilityPass) r.reasons.push(`field arm viable in fewer than ${minViable} blocks`);
    89	      } else {
    90	        r.mechanism = r.attribution.spaceP.passes && !r.attribution.spaceNP.passes ? "polymer-arrangement-attributed" : !r.attribution.spaceP.passes && r.attribution.spaceNP.passes ? "non-polymer field pattern" : "combined conditioned field";
    91	      }
    92	    }
    93	  }
    94	  return cands.map((c) => results.get(c.id)!);
    95	}
    96	
    97	export type PanelVerdictFe = "causal-benefit-detected" | "no-causal-benefit-detected" | "invalid-or-incomplete" | "unsupported-measurement";
    98	export interface PanelResultFe {
    99	  verdict: PanelVerdictFe;
   100	  text: string;
   101	  supported: string[];
   102	  negative: string[];
   103	  unusable: string[];
   104	}
   105	
   106	export function panelVerdictFe(results: readonly CandidateResultFe[]): PanelResultFe {
   107	  const supported = results.filter((r) => r.label === "supported").map((r) => r.id);
   108	  const negative = results.filter((r) => r.label === "unsupported-within-tested-domain").map((r) => r.id);
   109	  const unusable = results.filter((r) => r.label === "invalid-or-incomplete" || r.label === "unsupported-measurement").map((r) => r.id);
   110	  if (supported.length) return { verdict: "causal-benefit-detected", text: `Dependency edge supported for ${supported.join(", ")} within this domain.${unusable.length ? ` Not usable: ${unusable.join(", ")}.` : ""}`, supported, negative, unusable };
   111	  if (negative.length > 0 && unusable.length === 0) return { verdict: "no-causal-benefit-detected", text: `No causal benefit of size at least delta detected for ${negative.join(", ")} within the tested panel, horizon and field; the positive control qualified each.`, supported, negative, unusable };
   112	  const anyInvalid = results.some((r) => r.label === "invalid-or-incomplete");
   113	  return { verdict: anyInvalid ? "invalid-or-incomplete" : "unsupported-measurement", text: `No verdict: usable negatives ${negative.length ? negative.join(", ") : "none"}; not usable ${unusable.join(", ") || "none"}.`, supported, negative, unusable };
   114	}
   115	
   116	/** Exact P(Binomial(16, q) >= k) for q = qn/qd as a reduced-by-nothing rational of BigInts (tests compare it with the frozen power statement). */
   117	export function binomialUpperTail(n: number, k: number, qn: bigint, qd: bigint): { num: bigint; den: bigint } {
   118	  const choose = (a: number, b: number): bigint => {
   119	    let c = 1n;
   120	    for (let i = 1; i <= b; i++) c = (c * BigInt(a - b + i)) / BigInt(i);
   121	    return c;
   122	  };
   123	  let num = 0n;
   124	  for (let j = k; j <= n; j++) num += choose(n, j) * qn ** BigInt(j) * (qd - qn) ** BigInt(n - j);
   125	  return { num, den: qd ** BigInt(n) };
   126	}
   127	export { signTestP };
New inoculum contrast: `D_inoc2 = Y(b | builder) - max(Y(d1E), Y(d1B), Y(fU), Y(fS), Y(fB))`; every constructed comparator is in the max (none excluded by sign of gap; `comparator-excess` and `comparator-deficit` are labels in evidence). Rung-1 `D_inoc` is still computed and reported next to it. The four gating contrasts are builder, poly, space, inoc2. A comparator whose construction would need negative lattice E or exceed `POOL_MAX`/`MATTER_MAX` is refused (`credit-overdraw`, never clamped); a block where fU, fS and fB are all refused is `invalid-or-incomplete` for that candidate (reported, never dropped).

### 4.2 Better-powered readout (answers defect 2)

- **Replicated Stage 2.** Each arm's Stage 2 is run for `R = 4` sim seeds (block seed plus fixed offsets `{0, 1, 2, 3} * 1000`), identical offsets in every arm (so injury steps and RNG keys still coincide across arms); Stage 1 histories and transfers are shared. Primary endpoint `Y = sum over the R replicates of receiver-labelled B at T2` (integer; no division, units are x R of rung 1). Viability: receiver B above zero in at least `R - 1` of R replicates counts the field arm `b` as viable for that block. Viability is a requirement for the gate (field arm viable in at least 14 of 16 blocks; at a true per-block rate of 0.9, 15/16 would pass only 51% of the time). A paired survival contrast (arm vs comparator) is reported as attribution, never gating, and no survival benefit is claimed.
- **Replicate seeds** are enumerated and reserved explicitly (5.1) for the maximum R of 8.
- **More blocks:** 16 fresh blocks per candidate (was 8). Exact sign test over 16; `k = 16` gives 1/65,536, `k = 14` gives 137/65,536, `k = 12` gives 2,517/65,536 (hand-enumerated in tests). Candidate threshold by Holm at alpha 0.05 over 3 candidates as before (1/60, 1/40, 1/20 for the three ordered p-values).
- **Power statement (frozen wording, corrected at freeze after the Codex freeze review; see section 10).** With three candidates running the first Holm rejection needs k >= 13 of 16 (p = 697/65,536; 12 if only one candidate runs). A contrast whose true per-block positive probability is 0.75 passes that single threshold 40.5% of the time, 0.85 gives 79%, 0.90 gives 93%, 0.95 gives 99%; four contrasts, delta and viability can only lower the pass rate. A negative is a failure to detect a consistent benefit under these rules, not an exclusion bound on the effect size and not evidence of no benefit. Dev reports the empirical positive-block rate of the wall control at the qualifying dose next to this table.
- **Delta from a replicate control with the same readout:** `delta = max(ceil(0.02 * B_r * R), 1.5 * max|D|)` over dev replicate pairs (nonbuilder vs nonbuilder, builder vs builder, on a disjoint replicate seed set of R sims). With the sum over R the expected floor falls by about sqrt(R) relative to rung 1 after rescaling; dev says whether it did.
- Secondary readout (reported, never gating): per-replicate area under the receiver-B census curve (sum over the 8 censuses), as a lower-variance cross-check.
- **Noise gate (G-NOISE, dev only, before any fresh seed), per candidate:** `delta_c` must be at most half of the wall control's median effect on the **limiting gating contrast** (the one with the smallest median effect among the four), in summed-R units, at that candidate's qualifying dose (4.3). Otherwise the rung stops `unsupported-measurement` and reports the achieved floor and the minimum detectable dose; the fresh seeds are not read.

### 4.3 Positive control at realistic scale (answers defect 3)

The hand-wall fixtures (W1 ring, W2 floor under the receiver; rung-1 definitions) run the full four-contrast gate on dev wall seeds across a dose ladder of total polymer `P in {60, 120, 240, 600, 1200}`, ascending, stopping each wall at its first passing dose (that dose is its minimum detectable dose); doses above the ceiling are run only if nothing at or below it passes, to state the minimum detectable dose.

- **Per-candidate realistic ceiling** `C_c = 2 x` the median dev builder polymer at T1 of candidate c (rung 1 measured 84.5-103.5, so C is about 170-207, which admits 60 and 120, not 240). The control for candidate c uses doses `<= C_c` only.
- **Ordinary placement required.** The rung-1 `wallGateBlock` uses `allowP: true` (overwriting polymer on injured cells), which real transfers (`placeAll` default false) forbid. The qualifying control must place the wall by the ordinary placement rule (no overwrite of existing polymer, same pool-cap and reserve guards) at cells where that is legal; wall variants that need `allowP: true` stay as a reported diagnostic and never qualify the assay. If no ordinary-placement wall geometry exists, the control is `unavailable` and the stated reason is that none exists, ending the rung `unsupported-measurement`.
- **Wall pass rule (dev, 8 wall seeds):** for each of the four gating contrasts, at least 7 of 8 seeds positive (exact p = 9/256) and median >= `delta_c`. A wall passes at dose P iff all four hold. The control **qualifies candidate c** iff W1 or W2 passes at some dose `<= C_c` and G-NOISE holds (4.2).
- A candidate that does not qualify ends `unsupported-measurement` with the minimum detectable dose, its ratio to the builder polymer and the floor that explains it; its fresh blocks are not simulated (a sealed stopped payload is still written, section 8). Candidates qualify independently.

Dropped from this rung (cut for scope, section 11): the builder founder-biomass dose-response knob.

### 4.4 Readouts, ledger and flux (unchanged from rung 1, per replicate)

Per arm and replicate: receiver survival, receiver-labelled biomass inventory, flux attribution (synthesis share; "growth" only at share at least half and no saturated counter), builder cost, before/after inventories. Ledger invariant per arm: lattice + reserve + cumulative heatAnalytic against that arm's own starting total, exact `0n` at every census of both stages, including every replicate (the unchanged rung-1 audit; no credit term). Evidence adds per arm: free E, total energy, `constructionGap` vs `b`.

### 4.5 Fixtures

16x16, kernelRadius 5 at unit scale (rung-1 constraint); the positive control is additionally qualified at the frozen scale on dev.
- `far-field-energy` (new, 2.1: fools rung-1 `D_inoc`, not `D_inoc2`; full gate rejects via `D_poly = 0`); also a uniform and an in-footprint variant, `caughtBy` recorded.
- `free-gap-replay`: rung-1 observed direction at fixture scale (d0 holds MORE free E than `b` with equal totals; bound inventories as recorded); asserts fU/fS/fB reproduce `b`'s free E exactly (difference zero), that d0's excess is removed, and that `constructionGap` is recorded with every arm's own ledger residual `0n`.
- `takeover-only`, `null-field`, `hand-wall-gate-W2` (rung-1 fixtures re-run with the second protocol; expectations unchanged).
- Guards re-run: overwrite, pool cap, underfunded reserve, exhausted reserve, plus `credit-overdraw` (a free-matched comparator that would need negative lattice E or exceed a cap is refused, not clamped).

## 5. Pre-stated rules (frozen before any fresh-seed run; dated amendment in section 10)

### 5.1 Seeds
Block seeds in `8,601,001-8,601,999` and every derived replicate seed are reserved in `experiments/discovery/seed-registry.json` (owner `i06b free-energy-matched`) **before** anything runs. Replicate sims use block seed + `k * 1000`, `k = 0..7` (R = 4 uses k = 0..3; R = 8 uses k = 0..7), so derived seeds reach 8,608,999: the reservation lists the contiguous range `8,601,001-8,608,999`; the implementer first runs `seedCollisions` over it and, if any existing owner collides, replaces the offset by a collision-free stride and records that in the amendment. The protocol hash binds the explicit enumerated list of every seed that can run (block, replicate, noise, wall, fixture) and the registry digest; `--check` rescans the enumerated list, not only base lists. Never reused: 8,600,101-108, 8,600,501-508, nor any other rung-1 seed. Dev blocks 8,601,001-8,601,008, dev replicate-noise 8,601,201-8,601,204, wall 8,601,301-8,601,308, fixtures 8,601,401-8,601,408, fresh 8,601,501-8,601,516. Dev outcomes may set only `T1, T2` (unchanged unless dev shows cause), `delta_c`, the dose rungs and ceilings, and `R` (4 or 8; one escalation to 8 allowed if the noise gate fails, recorded before the freeze). Fresh seeds are read once, after the freeze commit is pushed and verified (section 9).

    28	  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'bl-site-niche-'));
    29	  try {
    30	    execFileSync('npx', ['vite', 'build', '--outDir', dir, '--emptyOutDir', '--logLevel', 'error'], { cwd: fileURLToPath(new URL('..', import.meta.url)), stdio: 'pipe' });
    31	    const rm = read('Dockerfile.site').match(/&& rm -rf (.+)/)?.[1];
    32	    expect(rm).toBeTruthy();
    33	    const files = (d: string): string[] => readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true }).flatMap((e) => e.isDirectory() ? files(join(d, e.name)) : [join(d, e.name)]);
    34	    const all = files(dir).map((f) => relative(dir, f));
    35	    const removed = new Set<string>();
    36	    for (const token of rm!.split(/\s+/)) {
    37	      const rel = token.replace(/^apps\/lab\/dist\//, '');
    38	      const re = new RegExp(`^${rel.replace(/[.+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&').replace(/\*/g, '[^/]*')}(/.*)?$`);
    39	      for (const f of all) if (re.test(f)) removed.add(f);
    40	    }
    41	    expect([...removed].some((f) => f.startsWith('niche/'))).toBe(true);
    42	    expect([...removed].some((f) => /^assets\/niche-.*\.js$/.test(f))).toBe(true);
    43	    const kept = new Set(all.filter((f) => !removed.has(f)));
    44	    const dangling: string[] = [];
    45	    for (const f of kept) {
    46	      if (!/\.(html|js|css)$/.test(f)) continue;
    47	      const text = readFileSync(join(dir, f), 'utf8');
    48	      for (const m of text.matchAll(/(?:\.\/|\/?assets\/)([A-Za-z0-9_.-]+\.(?:js|css))/g)) {
    49	        const target = `assets/${m[1]}`;
    50	        if (all.includes(target) && !kept.has(target)) dangling.push(`${f} -> ${target}`);
    51	      }
    52	    }
    53	    expect(dangling).toEqual([]);
    54	    expect(kept.has('lab/index.html')).toBe(true);
    55	  } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
    56	});
     1	// Pure helpers for the niche-construction page: formatting, labels, table rows. No DOM lookups; the page reads evidence.json only.
     2	export interface Rational { num: number; den: number }
     3	export const esc = (s: string) => s.replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' })[c]!);
     4	export const fmt = (n: number | null | undefined): string => (n === null || n === undefined ? 'n/a' : n.toLocaleString('en-US'));
     5	/** Twice-the-median integer to a readable half-step number, or n/a. */
     6	export const half = (n2: number | null | undefined): string => (n2 === null || n2 === undefined ? 'n/a' : n2 % 2 === 0 ? fmt(n2 / 2) : `${n2 < 0 ? '-' : ''}${fmt(Math.floor(Math.abs(n2) / 2))}.5`);
     7	export const ratText = (r: Rational): string => (r.num === r.den ? '1' : `${r.num}/${r.den}`);

     9	export type EdgeState = 'supported' | 'unsupported' | 'unavailable';
    10	export interface EdgeView { state: EdgeState; word: string; text: string }
    11	
    12	/** Evidence label -> the edge drawn between builder and receiver. A technical failure or a limited measurement is "unavailable", never "unsupported". */
    13	export function edgeOf(label: string, mechanism: string): EdgeView {
    14	  if (label === 'supported') return { state: 'supported', word: 'supported', text: `Dependency edge supported for this pair, horizon and field (${mechanism}).` };
    15	  if (label === 'unsupported-within-tested-domain') return { state: 'unsupported', word: 'unsupported', text: 'No causal benefit detected within the tested domain. This does not say a second link is impossible.' };
    16	  if (label === 'not-run') return { state: 'unavailable', word: 'unavailable', text: 'Not run (runtime rule). Not a negative result.' };
    17	  return { state: 'unavailable', word: 'unavailable', text: 'The assay could not decide (invalid blocks or an insensitive measurement). Not a negative result.' };
    18	}

    20	export const ARM_TEXT: Record<string, { name: string; what: string }> = {
    21	  a: { name: 'Co-culture', what: 'The builder is still alive when the receiver arrives.' },
    22	  b: { name: 'Builder removed', what: 'Living cells are relabelled non-living; the whole conditioned field stays in place.' },
    23	  c: { name: 'Matched inventory, scrambled', what: 'Same totals of every channel as the builder-removed field, structure shuffled.' },
    24	  cP: { name: 'Polymer only scrambled', what: 'Only the polymer is shuffled; everything else stays where it was.' },
    25	  cNP: { name: 'Everything but polymer scrambled', what: 'All other channels are shuffled; the polymer stays where it was.' },
    26	  nb: { name: 'Nonbuilder history', what: 'Same founders and budget, without the building gene, then removed.' },
    27	  ab: { name: 'Ablation', what: 'Builder cost kept; its polymer is turned into waste at the transfer, the released energy booked as named heat.' },
    28	  d0: { name: 'Receiver alone', what: 'The same total budget as an unconditioned lattice, same inoculum.' },
    29	  d1E: { name: 'Receiver alone + field energy', what: 'Receiver alone with the free energy the field held near the inoculum handed over as inoculum.' },
    30	  d1B: { name: 'Receiver alone + field matter', what: 'Receiver alone with the bound matter the field held near the inoculum handed over as inoculum.' },
    31	};

    41	export interface ArmRow { id: string; name: string; what: string; /** twice the median, or null when no block measured the arm */ median2: number | null; /** null = the block did not measure this arm (invalid block) */ perBlock: (number | null)[]; survived: string }
    42	export function armRows(blocks: { outcomes?: Record<string, { y: number }> }[], medianYTimes2: Record<string, number | null>, survivedByArm: Record<string, { survived: number; of: number }> = {}): ArmRow[] {
    43	  return ARM_ORDER.filter((id) => id in medianYTimes2).map((id) => ({
    44	    id, ...ARM_TEXT[id], median2: medianYTimes2[id], perBlock: blocks.map((b) => b.outcomes?.[id]?.y ?? null),
    45	    survived: survivedByArm[id] ? `${survivedByArm[id].survived} of ${survivedByArm[id].of}` : 'n/a',
    46	  }));
    47	}

    49	export interface LedgerRow { arm: string; matter: string; energy: string; reserve: string; residual: string }
    50	export function ledgerRows(startTotals: Record<string, { matter: string; energy: string; reserveB: number; reserveE: number }> | undefined, transfers: Record<string, { residual: string; matterResidual: string }[]> | undefined): LedgerRow[] {
    51	  const st = startTotals ?? {};
    52	  return ARM_ORDER.filter((id) => id in st).map((arm) => {
    53	    const t = st[arm];
    54	    const bad = ((transfers ?? {})[arm] ?? []).some((r) => r.residual !== '0' || r.matterResidual !== '0');
    55	    return { arm, matter: t.matter, energy: t.energy, reserve: `B ${t.reserveB}, E ${t.reserveE}`, residual: bad ? 'NONZERO' : '0' };
    56	  });
    57	}

    63	/** Scan every transfer of every block in the payload: the page states the residual claim from this, never from static text. */
    64	export function residualScan(ev: EvidenceView): { blocks: number; transfers: number; nonzero: number } {
    65	  const blocks = [...(ev.dev?.candidates ?? []), ...(ev.fresh?.candidates ?? [])].flatMap((c) => c.blocks ?? []);
    66	  let transfers = 0, nonzero = 0;
    67	  for (const b of blocks) for (const recs of Object.values(b.transfers ?? {})) for (const r of recs) { transfers++; if (r.residual !== '0' || r.matterResidual !== '0') nonzero++; }
    68	  return { blocks: blocks.length, transfers, nonzero };
    69	}

    99	export function verdictHtml(ev: EvidenceView): string {
   100	  const f = ev.fresh;
   101	  const stopped = f.ran ? '' : '<p class="verdict-stop"><strong>Stopped before the fresh-seed run.</strong> No confirmation block was run, so there are no arms, contrasts or ledgers below to read.</p>';

codex
No P1 findings. The stopped `unsupported-measurement` verdict is supported; I found three P2 and three P3 issues in the new tests and presentation.

1. **P2 — Published evidence claims the failed control demonstrated detection.**  
   [evidence.json:31641](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i06-free-energy-matched/docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/evidence.json:31641) says W1 “shows the assay can detect a polymer structure of that dose,” and [page.ts:22](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i06-free-energy-matched/apps/lab/src/niche/page.ts:22) displays it verbatim. Every qualification is false and every minimum detectable dose is null. Readers therefore receive contradictory sensitivity claims. **Fix:** use conditional wording—“A passing control would demonstrate…”—and explicitly describe this run’s failure. Record any correction to the sealed artifact transparently without changing frozen decision rules.

2. **P2 — The positive fixture test cannot produce the required supported result.**  
   [niche-fe-fixtures.test.ts:54](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i06-free-energy-matched/packages/runner/test/niche-fe-fixtures.test.ts:54) truncates W2 to four seeds and only checks contrast names. Even 4/4 positive gives p = 1/16, above 0.05; this test cannot demonstrate `supported`. A regression eliminating the wall effect would pass. PLAN section 7 explicitly requires `hand-wall-gate-W2 supported`; the saved eight-seed fixture achieves it. **Fix:** run all eight seeds and assert `fullGateLabel === "supported"` and no failed gating contrasts.

3. **P2 — The verifying-lock acceptance test accepts verification failures.**  
   [niche-fe-evidence.test.ts:73](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i06-free-energy-matched/tools/test/niche-fe-evidence.test.ts:73) checks only `Array.isArray(await verifyLock())` when the lock exists. Both successful verification and a list of serious lock errors satisfy this assertion. The surrounding remote/dirty-tree tests do not exercise malformed-lock refusal. **Fix:** add missing/corrupt protocol, calibration and source-binding cases, and assert the generator rejects before simulation; require an empty error list for the valid lock.

4. **P3 — The noise-reduction test permits no reduction or a dead fixture.**  
   [niche-fe-readout.test.ts:97](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i06-free-energy-matched/packages/runner/test/niche-fe-readout.test.ts:97) asserts `f4 <= 4*f1`, although PLAN section 7 requires “below.” Constant endpoints yield `f1 = f4 = 0` and pass. **Fix:** require `f1 > 0` and strict `<`. The current fixture supports stronger assertions: my replay produced `f1 = 231`, `f4 = 500`.

5. **P3 — The page’s qualification rule omits G-NOISE.**  
   [index.html:48](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i06-free-energy-matched/apps/lab/niche/index.html:48) says a passing dose within the polymer ceiling qualifies a candidate. Frozen qualification also requires the limiting median to reach **2 × delta**. A within-ceiling wall with median `1.5 × delta` passes the stated wall rule but correctly fails qualification. **Fix:** add G-NOISE and specify that the ceiling uses twice the median dev builder polymer.

6. **P3 — The replicate-survival boundary is not tested.**  
   [niche-fe-readout.test.ts:151](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i06-free-energy-matched/packages/runner/test/niche-fe-readout.test.ts:151) tests supplied block-viability counts, rather than the separate `R−1 of R` conversion required by PLAN section 7. Requiring 4/4 survivors, or accepting 2/4, would escape this test; saved dev blocks all have four survivors. **Fix:** exercise R=4 with two, three and four surviving replicates, expecting false/true/true viability.
